------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE abrecupom
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE abrecupom
Lparameters oFormulario

Local llRetorno

If CrSigOpCdd.PafEcfs = 1 And Used('CrSigFiMpf') And InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'BEMA8501','DARUMA','NSWEDA','EPSON')
	If ! thisform.parentform.AtuaGt(0,.f.,.t.)
		Return .f.
	EndIf
Endif

lcCliente = (TprMvCab.Contads)
=ThisForm.PoDataMgr.CursorQuery('SigCdCli','CrTmpCli','Iclis',lcCliente,[Cpfs,Rclis,paises,Endes,Nums,cidas,Estas,Rgs,Compls])

lcCpf = ''
lcNom = ''
lcEnd = ''
If InList(crSigCdOpe.ImpClis,[S],[O]) && mena - 04/04/2014 para nao sair cnpj, deixar em branco no cadastro --  and !EMPTY(ThisForm.Cnt_Identifica.Destino.Get_cpf.Value)	&& mena 27/03/2014 - se usu nao digitou cpf/cnpj nao imprime
	If CrSigOpCdd.ConfIcs <> 1 Or MessageBox('Deseja Imprimir o CPF/CNPJ do Cliente? ',4+32+256,'Confirmação') = 6
		lcCpf = CrTmpCli.Cpfs
		lcNom = Alltrim(CrTmpCli.Rclis)
		lcEnd = Alltrim(CrTmpCli.Endes)+' '+Alltrim(CrTmpCli.Nums)+' '+Alltrim(CrTmpCli.compls)+' '+Alltrim(CrTmpCli.Cidas)+' '+Alltrim(CrTmpCli.Estas)
	Endif
EndIf

llRetorno = AbrirCupom(oFormulario,lcCpf,lcNom,lcEnd)

If Used('crTefImp')
	Select crTefImp
	Zap
Endif

Return (llRetorno)

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE atuacupom
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE atuacupom
Lparameters vTipo,vnItem

Local LnCodBarra,lcDescr

Thisform.CaixaLivre.Visible = .F.

If Not vTipo

	lcDescr = crTpmMvItn.Dpros
	lcSql = [Select Cpros,Cbars,DescFis,DescEcfs From SigCdPro Where Cpros = ']+crTpmMvItn.Cpros+[']
	=Thisform.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'xPro')

	If CrSigOpCdd.IdentPro = 1 And CrSigCdOpe.OpCrs <> 4
		_Memo = Set( 'Memo' )
		Set Memo To 40
		lcDescr = Padr(fLimpaTexto( Mline( xPro.DescFis, 1 ) ),40)
		Set Memo To _Memo
	Else
		If Not IsEmpty( crTpmMvItn.CodBarras)
			LnCodBarra = crTpmMvItn.CodBarras
		Else
			lcSql = [Select Cpros,Cbars,DescEcfs From SigCdPro Where Cpros = ']+crTpmMvItn.Cpros+[']
			=Thisform.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'xPro')
			lnNotas = Val( crTpmMvItn.Notas )
			If CrSigCdOpe.OpCrs = 4 And Not IsEmpty( Alltrim( crTpmMvItn.Notas ) ) And ;
					( Transform( lnNotas, '@L 999999' ) = crTpmMvItn.Notas )
				LnCodBarra = lnNotas
			Else
				LnCodBarra = xPro.CBars
			Endif
		Endif
	Endif
	If Not Empty(xPro.DescEcfs)
		lcDescr = xPro.DescEcfs
	Endif

	Select crTpmMvItnC
	Delete For cItens >= 1000
	If Type('vnItem') = "N" And vnItem > 0
		xTexto1 = '..Cancelamento do Item: '+Transform(vnItem,"@L 999")
		Insert Into crTpmMvItnC (cItens, Linha, Texto ) Values ;
			(Thisform.Citem - 1,4,xTexto1)
	Else
		If Not Seek(Str(crTpmMvItn.cItens,4))
			xTexto1 = Transform(crTpmMvItn.cItens,"@Z 9999")+' '+Padr(Iif(CrSigFiFis.Imps='S/IMPRESSORA',crTpmMvItn.Cpros,Padl(LnCodBarra,14,'0')),14)+' '+lcDescr
			Insert Into crTpmMvItnC (cItens, Linha, Texto ) Values ;
				(crTpmMvItn.cItens,1,xTexto1)
				
			If Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.ValDescs)
				xTexto2 = '    '+Transform(crTpmMvItn.Qtds,"@Z 999.99")+' '+crTpmMvItn.cunis+' X  '+;
					Transform(crTpmMvItn.UniVals,"@Z 999,999,999.99")+' '+;
					Transform(crTpmMvItn.UniVals*crTpmMvItn.Qtds,"@Z 999,999,999.99")
				Insert Into crTpmMvItnC (cItens, Linha, Texto ) Values ;
					(crTpmMvItn.cItens,2,xTexto2)
				
				xTexto2 = '    '+Iif(crTpmMvItn.ValDescs<0,[Acrescimo],[Desconto ])+Transform(crTpmMvItn.ValDescs *(-1),"@Z 999,999,999.99") + ' ' +;
					Transform(crTpmMvItn.Totas,"@Z 999,999,999.99")
				Insert Into crTpmMvItnC (cItens, Linha, Texto ) Values ;
					(crTpmMvItn.cItens,2,xTexto2)
					
			ELSE
			
				xTexto2 = '    '+Transform(crTpmMvItn.Qtds,"@Z 999.99")+' '+crTpmMvItn.cunis+' X  '+;
					Transform(crTpmMvItn.Units,"@Z 999,999,999.99")+' '+;
					Transform(crTpmMvItn.Totas,"@Z 999,999,999.99")
				Insert Into crTpmMvItnC (cItens, Linha, Texto ) Values ;
					(crTpmMvItn.cItens,2,xTexto2)

			Endif
		Endif
	Endif
Else
	Select [ ] As Agrupar, Sum(Qtds) As QtItem From crTpmMvItn Into Cursor TotItem Group By 1
	_cit = 1004
	Select crTpmMvItnC
	Delete For cItens >= 1000

	xTexto  = Replicate('-',47)
	xTexto0 = 'TI:'+Transform(TotItem.QtItem,"@z 9,999.99")
	xTexto1 = ''
	xTexto2 = Padr('..TOTAL',33)+Transform(Thisform.Cnt_fpagto.Get_totLiquido.Value,"@Z 999,999,999.99")

	Insert Into crTpmMvItnC (cItens, Linha, Texto ) Values ;
		(1001,0,xTexto)
	Insert Into crTpmMvItnC (cItens, Linha, Texto, Cores ) Values ;
		(1001,1,xTexto0, 1)
	Insert Into crTpmMvItnC (cItens, Linha, Texto, Cores ) Values ;
		(1002,1,xTexto1, 1)
	Insert Into crTpmMvItnC (cItens, Linha, Texto, Cores ) Values ;
		(1003,2,xTexto2, 1)

	Select nChaves, fPags, Trocos, cnIdTefs, Infos, Max(tParcs) As tParcs, Max(pDescs) As pDescs, ;
		Sum(Valos) As Valos, Sum(ValDescs) As ValDescs, Sum(Bcds) As Bcds ;
		From xPar Into Cursor xParECF Group By 1,2,3,4,5 Order By nChaves,fPags

	_ttBcd = 0
	Select xParECF
	Scan
		xTexto = Padr('..'+xParECF.fPags+Iif(Not Inlist(xParECF.Infos,[T],[C]),'',' ('+Transform(xParECF.tParcs,"@L 99")+'x)'+' '+Iif(xParECF.tParcs=0,'',Transform(xParECF.Valos/xParECF.tParcs,"@Z 9999,999.99"))),33)+Transform(xParECF.Valos,"@Z 999,999,999.99")
		Insert Into crTpmMvItnC (cItens, Linha, Texto, Cores ) Values ;
			(_cit,1,xTexto, Iif(xParECF.Trocos=1,4,2) )
		_cit = _cit + 1
		If xParECF.ValDescs <> 0
			xTexto = Padr(Space(10)+'Desconto '+Alltrim(Transform(xParECF.pDescs,"@Z 9999.9999%")),33)+Transform(xParECF.ValDescs,"@Z 999,999,999.99")
			Insert Into crTpmMvItnC (cItens, Linha, Texto, Cores ) Values ;
				(_cit,1,xTexto, 2 )
			_cit = _cit + 1
		Endif
		_ttBcd = _ttBcd + xParECF.Bcds
		Thisform.GrdCupom.Refresh
	Endscan
	Thisform.Bcds = _ttBcd
	Select xPar
	Go Bott

	If Thisform.Cnt_fpagto.get_totForma.Value <> 0
		xTexto = Padr('..Valor Recebido',33)+Transform(Thisform.Cnt_fpagto.get_totForma.Value,"@Z 999,999,999.99")
		Insert Into crTpmMvItnC (cItens, Linha, Texto, Cores ) Values ;
			(2000,1,xTexto,3)
	Endif
	If Thisform.Cnt_fpagto.Get_totLiquido.Value < Thisform.Cnt_fpagto.get_totForma.Value And ;
			ThisForm.Cnt_fpagto.Get_totLiquido.Value > 0 And Thisform.Libtroco
		xTroco = Thisform.Cnt_fpagto.get_totForma.Value - Thisform.Cnt_fpagto.Get_totLiquido.Value
		xTexto = Padr('..TROCO',33)+Transform(xTroco,"@Z 999,999,999.99")
		Insert Into crTpmMvItnC (cItens, Linha, Texto, Cores ) Values ;
			(3000,1,xTexto,4)
	Endif
Endif
Select crTpmMvItnC
Thisform.GrdCupom.Refresh

Thisform.LockScreen = .T.
=fCarregaGrade(Thisform.GrdCupom)
Thisform.LockScreen = .F.
Thisform.Cnt_mensagem.Mensagem.Caption = ''
Select crTpmMvItn

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE atualizatotal
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE atualizatotal
ctotal=0
Select xPar
Scan
	=ThisForm.Parentform.podatamgr.cursorquery('SigOpFp','CrSigOpFp','Fpags',xPar.Fpags)
	cotacao = ThisForm.ParentForm.Cotacao( xpar.moefpgs )
	cotoper = 1
	If crSigOpFp.Trocos = 1
		ctotal = Round( ctotal - ( xpar.valos * xpar.cotfpgs / cotoper ), 2 )
	Else
		ctotal = Round( ctotal + ( xpar.valos * xpar.cotfpgs / cotoper ), 2 )
	Endif
Endscan
With Thisform.Cnt_Fpagto
	ThisForm.CalculaDesconto()
	_Enc = 0
	lnvTot = ThisForm.AtuaTotal() + .Acrescimo.Get_TotVariação.Value - .Desconto.Get_TotVariação.Value - .Get_Tvariacao.Value
	Thisform.Parentform.pagina.dados.pgfprincipal.pgoperacional.getvaltos.value = lnvTot
	.Get_TotLiquido.Value = lnvTot
	.Get_TotForma.Value = ctotal
	.Get_Saldo.Value = .Get_TotLiquido.Value - .Get_totforma.value
Endwith
Return .t.
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE atuatotal
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE atuatotal
Local _Valos

_valos   = TprMvCab.valinis + CrSigMvNfi.VlFretes + CrSigMvNfi.Vlseguros + CrSigMvNfi.DespAces
Return (_Valos)


ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE calculadesconto
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE calculadesconto
Lparameters pSubNivel

Local ValDsc, lnReg

With ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto

	If CrSigOpCdd.Varps = 1

		Select xPar
		Sum ValDescs to ValDsc
		Go Bottom

		.Get_tvariacao.Value = ValDsc
		Replace ValVarPs With ValDsc * -1 in TprMvCab

		lnvVar = .Acrescimo.Get_TotVariação.Value - .Desconto.Get_TotVariação.Value - .Get_tvariacao.Value
		_Total = ThisForm.AtuaTotal()
		_Varia = IIf(_Total=0,0,Round( Abs(lnvVar) / _Total * 100, 4 ))

		If lnvVar < 0
			Replace ValVars With -Abs(lnvVar) ,;
					Vars    With -Abs(_Varia) in TprMvCab
		Else
			Replace ValVars With Abs(lnvVar) ,;
					Vars    With Abs(_Varia) in TprMvCab
		Endif
		_Enc = 0
		lnvTot = ThisForm.AtuaTotal() + .Acrescimo.Get_TotVariação.Value - .Desconto.Get_TotVariação.Value - .Get_Tvariacao.Value
		Thisform.Parentform.pagina.dados.pgfprincipal.pgoperacional.getvaltos.value = lnvTot
		.Get_TotLiquido.Value = lnvTot
	EndIf
	If pSubNivel
		Select ParcTef
		lnReg = Recno()
		Sum (Valos)*Iif(Trocos=1,-1,1) To x
		Sum (ValDescs)*Iif(Trocos=1,-1,1) For Baixado <> 1 To w
		Sum ValDescs to ValDsc
		Sum Valos+ValDescs to Valbcd For ValDescs <> 0
		ThisForm.Bcds = Valbcd
		_Total = ThisForm.Atuatotal() + .Acrescimo.Get_TotVariação.Value - .Desconto.Get_TotVariação.Value 
		_Varia = IIf(_Total=0,0,Round( ValDsc / _Total * 100, 4 ))

		Thisform.TotParTef = .Get_TotLiquido.Value - x - w

		Select ParcTef
		Locate For Recno() = lnReg
	EndIf
	.Get_Saldo.Value = .Get_TotLiquido.Value - .Get_totforma.value
	.Get_TotLiquido.Refresh
	.Get_Saldo.Refresh

EndWith

Return .t.

ENDPROC

* PROCEDURE calc_valite
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE calc_valite
=ThisForm.ParentForm.Calc_ValIte()

ENDPROC

* PROCEDURE cancelcupom
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE cancelcupom
Thisform.Subtotac = .t.
oFormulario = Thisform.Parentform.oModemEest
ThisForm.Cnt_mensagem.Mensagem.Caption = 'Verificando Totalização...'
_ValCupFis = VerSubt(oFormulario)
ThisForm.Cnt_mensagem.Mensagem.Caption = ' '
If _ValcupFis = TprMvCab.Valinis
	Return .f.
Else
	Return .t.
EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE cmdenviaprod
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE cmdenviaprod
If Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_subtotal.Get_Valor.Value # 0
	ThisForm.Cnt_mensagem.Mensagem.Caption = 'Imprimindo Itens...'
	
	If Not Thisform.ItemFiscal()
		If ThisForm.CancelCupom()
			Thisform.mExcluiItem()
			ThisForm.Total_item()
		Else
			Select crTpmMvItn
			Replace ImpCupFis with .t.
			ThisForm.AtuaCupom()
			ThisForm.ParentForm.CancelaItem = .T.
			ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Apaga.Enabled = .T.
		Endif
	Else
		Select crTpmMvItn
		Replace ImpCupFis with .t.
		ThisForm.AtuaCupom()
		ThisForm.ParentForm.CancelaItem = .T.
		ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Apaga.Enabled = .T.
	Endif
	Thisform.mLimpaCntItem(.T.)
Else
	Messagebox('Valor do Produto Inválido !!',0+48,'')
	Thisform.mExcluiItem()
	Thisform.mLimpaCntItem( .T. )
	Thisform.MontaTela()
Endif

ThisForm.Cnt_mensagem.Mensagem.caption = ''
Thisform.mLimpaCntItem()
Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Produto.Get_Produto.SetFocus

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE gerarcupom
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE gerarcupom
Local lnTenta, lcNocupom, lcNoCcf, lnNoCcf, lnNoCupom

oFormulario = Thisform.Parentform.oModemEest

lnTenta = 2
DO While lnTenta > 0
	lcNoCupom = ''
	lcNoCupom = LeituraNoCupom(oFormulario)
	If type('lcNoCupom')='C' And Not Empty(lcNoCupom)
		Do Case
			Case  Inlist(crSigFiFis.Imps,'SWEDA','NSWEDA','BEMATECH','ELGIN','BEMA8501','DARUMA','S/IMPRESSORA','EPSON')
				lnNoCupom = val(lcNoCupom)
				If InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'BEMATECH','BEMA8501','EPSON') And crSigCdOpe.CupFis=3
					lnNoCupom = lnNoCupom + 1
				Endif
			Case  InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'DATAREGIS','NDATAREGIS')
				lnNoCupom = val(lcNoCupom) + 1
		EndCase
		lcNoCupom = Transform(lnNoCupom,'@L 999999')
		lnTenta   = 0
	Else
		lnTenta = lnTenta - 1
		ldtime = DateTime()
		Do While DateTime() - ldtime <= 1	&& mena 20/11/2014 diminui tempo 2
			=chrsaw(.5)
		EndDo
		lcNoCupom = []
	EndIf
	If Val(lcNoCupom) = 0 And lnTenta = 0 And Messagebox('Impressora Não Responde (50), Tentar Novamente ?',4+48,'Leitura do No.Fabricacao') = 6
		lnTenta = 2
	Endif
EndDo
If Val(lcNoCupom) = 0
	Return .f.
EndIf

lnTenta = 2
DO While lnTenta > 0

	If CrSigCdOpe.CupFis = 3
		lcNoccf	= LeituraNoGnf()
	Else
		lcNoCcf = LeituraNoCCf()
	Endif
	lnNoCcf = 0
	If type('lcNoccf')='C' And Not Empty(lcNoccf)
		Do Case
			Case  Inlist(crSigFiFis.Imps,'SWEDA','NSWEDA','BEMATECH','ELGIN','BEMA8501','DARUMA','S/IMPRESSORA','EPSON')
				lnNoccf = val(lcNoccf)
				If InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'BEMATECH','BEMA8501','NSWEDA') And crSigCdOpe.CupFis=3
					lnNoccf = lnNoccf + 1
				Endif
				If InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'DARUMA') And crSigCdOpe.CupFis=3
					lnModelo = ImpModelo()
					If lnModelo = 3
						lnNoccf = lnNoccf + 1
					Endif
				Endif
			Case  InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'DATAREGIS','NDATAREGIS')
				lnNoccf = val(lcNoccf) + 1
		EndCase
		lcNoccf = Transform(lnNoccf,'@L 999999')
		lnTenta   = 0
	Else
		lnTenta = lnTenta - 1
		ldtime = DateTime()
		Do While DateTime() - ldtime <= 1	&& mena 20/11/2014 diminui tempo 2
			=chrsaw(.5)
		EndDo
		lcNoCcf = []
	EndIf
	If Val(lcNoccf) = 0 And lnTenta = 0 And Messagebox('Impressora Não Responde (50), Tentar Novamente ?',4+48,'Leitura do No.'+Iif(CrSigCdOpe.CupFis=3,'GNF','CCF')) = 6
		lnTenta = 2
	EndIf
EndDo
If Val(lcNoccf) = 0
	Return .f.
EndIf

Select TprMvCab
Replace notas   With lcNoCupom ,;
		nCupoms With lcNoCupom ,;
		ccfgnfs With lcNoCCf In TprMvCab

Return (.T.)

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE impcheque
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE impcheque
If ! crSigFiMpF.Pertos
	=ThisForm.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdEmp','CrTmpEmp','Cemps',_Empr,[Cidas,razsocs])

	fMensagemFixa( "Posicione o cheque na impressora...", 48, "IMPRIME CHEQUE", .F., .t., "\VBMP\a_print4.bmp" )

	lcCidade = fLimpaTexto(Alltrim( CrTmpEmp.Cidas ))
	lcFavo = Iif(CrSigFiMpf.Nominal,fLimpaTexto(Alltrim(CrTmpEmp.Razsocs)),'')
	ldData = Iif(Gomonth(Date(),CrSigCdPac.MesVencs) < xPar.Vencs Or CrSigCdPac.MesVencs = 0,xPar.Vencs,Date())

	RetImpChe = ImpChe(xpar.Bancos,xpar.Valos,lcFavo,lcCidade,ldData, Thisform.parentform.oModemEest)
	
	If CrSigFiMpf.BomPara And Ttod(xpar.Vencs) # Date() And MessageBox('Confirma Impressão Bom Para',4+32,'Imprime Cheque') = 6
		fMensagemFixa( "Posicione o cheque na impressora...", 48, "IMPRIME BOM PARA", .F., .t., "\VBMP\a_print4.bmp" )
		RetImpChe = BomPara(Dtoc(Ttod(xpar.Vencs)),ThisForm.parentform.oModemEest)
	Endif
Else
	If ThisForm.ParentForm.pImpcheque And !Empty(xpar.Bancos) And !Empty(xpar.Agencias) And !Empty(xpar.Contas) And !Empty(xPar.Numeros)
		If MessageBox('Confirma Impressão do Cheque',4+32,'Imprime Cheque') = 6
			If Not Empty(CrCfComItc.Portas)
				If CrCfComItc.TpLeChqs = "K"
					llRet = PertoHabilita(crCfComitc.Portas,Iif(crCfComitc.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
					If llRet = 1
						llRet = PertoData(Iif(Gomonth(Date(),CrSigCdPac.MesVencs) < xPar.Vencs Or CrSigCdPac.MesVencs = 0,xPar.Vencs,Date()),Iif(crCfComitc.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
					Else
						llRet = .f.
					Endif
					ThisForm.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdEmp','CrTmpEmp','Cemps',_Empr,[Cidas,Razsocs])
					If llRet 
						lcCidade = fLimpaTexto(Alltrim( CrTmpEmp.Cidas ))
						llRet = PertoCidade(lcCidade,Iif(crCfComitc.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
					Endif
					If llRet 
						lcFavo = Iif(CrSigFiMpf.Nominal,fLimpaTexto(Alltrim(CrTmpEmp.Razsocs)),'')
						llRet = PertoBenef(lcFavo,Iif(crCfComitc.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
					Endif
					If llRet 
						If crSigFiMpF.BomPara And xPar.Vencs > Date()
							llRet = PertoBomPara(xPar.Vencs,Iif(crCfComitc.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
						Else
							llRet = Perto20Ger(Space(20),Iif(crCfComitc.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
						Endif
					EndIf
					If llRet And crSigFiMpF.Cruzado
						llRet = PertoCruzamento('3','','','',Iif(crCfComitc.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
					EndIf
					fMensagemFixa( "Posicione o cheque na impressora...", 48, "IMPRIME CHEQUE", .F., .t., "\VBMP\a_print4.bmp" )
					If llRet 
						If crSigFiMpF.Cruzado
							llRet = PertoPreenche('8',xpar.Valos,xpar.Bancos,.f.,.t.,Iif(crCfComitc.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
						Else
							llRet = PertoPreenche('0',xpar.Valos,xpar.Bancos,.f.,.t.,Iif(crCfComitc.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
						Endif
					Endif
					PertoDesabilita(Iif(crCfComitc.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
				Else
					llRet = ChronoHabilita(crCfComitc.Portas)
					If llRet
						llRet = ChronoData(crCfComitc.Portas,Iif(Gomonth(Date(),CrSigCdPac.MesVencs) < xPar.Vencs Or CrSigCdPac.MesVencs = 0,xPar.Vencs,Date()))
					Else
						llRet = .f.
					Endif
					ThisForm.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdEmp','CrTmpEmp','Cemps',_Empr,[Cidas,Razsocs])
					If llRet 
						lcCidade = fLimpaTexto(Alltrim( CrTmpEmp.Cidas ))
						llRet = ChronoCidade(crCfComitc.Portas,lcCidade)
					Endif
					If llRet 
						lcFavo = Iif(CrSigFiMpf.Nominal,fLimpaTexto(Alltrim(CrTmpEmp.Razsocs)),'')
						llRet = ChronoBenef(crCfComitc.Portas, lcFavo)
					Endif
					fMensagemFixa( "Posicione o cheque na impressora...", 48, "IMPRIME CHEQUE", .F., .t., "\VBMP\a_print4.bmp" )
					If llRet 
						llRet = ChronoBanco(crCfComitc.Portas,xpar.Bancos)
						llRet = ChronoValor(crCfComitc.Portas,xpar.Valos)
						llRet = ChronoPreenche(crCfComitc.Portas)
					Endif
					ChronoDesabilita(crCfComitc.Portas)
				EndIf
			Endif
		EndIf
	Endif
Endif

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE impitemcan
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE impitemcan
*** homologação paf - 09/2012 imprime e cancela item cancelado no DAV ***
SELECT crTpmMvItn
GO top

=ThisForm.PoDataMgr.CursorQuery('SigExMvi','crTpmMvItne','empdopnums',crTpmMvItn.empdopnums,[*])

ImpVen=.T.
Select crTpmMvItnE
Go Top
Do While !eof() And ImpVen
	If CrSigCdOpe.CupFis = 1 And crSigCdOpe.digitens = 1 And crSigFiFis.Imps <> 'BEMATECH'
		ThisForm.SubTotac = .t.
	Endif
	=ThisForm.PoDataMgr.CursorQuery('SigCdPro','CrTmpPro','Cpros',crTpmMvItnE.cpros,[Cpros,Cbars,Icms,Cgrus,tptribs,SitTrIcms,cUnis,DescFis,DescEcfs,IdPro])
	If ImpVen
		If CrSigOpCdd.IdentPro = 1 
			_Memo = Set( 'Memo' )
			Set Memo to 40
			lcDescr = Padr(fLimpaTexto(MLine( CrTmpPro.DescFis, 1 ) ),40)
			Set Memo to _Memo
		Else
			CodBarra = CrTmpPro.Cbars
			lcDescr  = fLimpaTexto(crTpmMvItnE.Dpros)
		Endif
		If Not Empty(CrTmpPro.DescEcfs)
			lcDescr = CrTmpPro.DescEcfs
		Endif

		lcTipo  = CrSigFiTri.Tipos
		If CrSigFiFis.Imps = 'NSWEDA' And InList(CrSigFiTri.Tipos,'F','I','N')
			lcTipo = Padr(CrSigFiTri.Tipos,3)
		EndIf
		If crSigCdOpe.ChkEDesc = 1
			If InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'DATAREGIS','NDATAREGIS')
				lnPDesc = round((crTpmMvItnE.Valdescs/(crTpmMvItnE.Units+crTpmMvItnE.Valdescs)) * 100,2)
				ImpVen=VenFis(CodBarra,lcDescr,crSigFiTri.Ordems,crTpmMvItnE.Qtds,crTpmMvItnE.Units+crTpmMvItnE.ValDescs,lnPDesc,oFormulario,lcTipo,.f.,Left(CrTmpPro.Cunis,2))
				ImpVen=cancelItemUlt(oFormulario)
			Else
				ImpVen=VenFis(CodBarra,lcDescr,crSigFiTri.Ordems,crTpmMvItnE.Qtds,crTpmMvItnE.Units+crTpmMvItnE.ValDescs,crTpmMvItnE.ValDescs*crTpmMvItnE.Qtds,oFormulario,lctipo,.f.,Left(CrTmpPro.Cunis,2))
				ImpVen=cancelItemUlt(oFormulario)
			Endif
		Else
			ImpVen=VenFis(CodBarra,lcDescr,crSigFiTri.Ordems,crTpmMvItnE.Qtds,crTpmMvItnE.Units,0,oFormulario,lctipo,.f.,Left(CrTmpPro.Cunis,2))
			ImpVen=cancelItemUlt(oFormulario)
		EndIf
		m.CItens = crTpmMvItnE.citens
		With ThisForm.Mensagems
			xTexto1 = Padl(crTpmMvItnE.Cpros,14,'0')+' '+crTpmMvItnE.Dpros
			xTexto2 = Transform(crTpmMvItnE.Qtds,"@Z 999.99")+' '+crTpmMvItnE.cunis+' X '+;
					  Transform(crTpmMvItnE.Units,"@Z 999,999.99")+' = '+;
					  Transform(crTpmMvItnE.Totas,"@Z 999,999.99")
			.mensage1.Caption = xTexto1
			.mensage2.Caption = xTexto2
			.Refresh
		EndWith
		ThisForm.Atuacupom()
		If Not ImpVen
			ImpVen = .t.
			DO While .t.
				llOk = 7
				DO While llOk = 7
					llOk = Messagebox('Impressora Não Responde (51), Tentar Novamente ?',4+32+256,'')
					ThisForm.Subtotac = .t.
				EndDo
				If InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'BEMATECH','BEMA8501','DARUMA','EPSON')
					VerStatus = RotStatus('NOK',oFormulario)
					If Type('VerStatus')<>'L' And Not Empty(VerStatus)
						Exit
					EndIf
				Else
					If InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'DATAREGIS','NDATAREGIS')
						VerStatus = RotStatus(.t.,oFormulario)
						If Type('VerStatus')<>'L' And Substr(VerStatus,2,1) = 'S'
							Exit
						EndIf
					Else
						If InList(CrSigFiFis.Imps,'SWEDA','NSWEDA')
							VerStatus = RotStatus(.t.,oFormulario)
							If Type('VerStatus')<>'L' And Not Empty(VerStatus)
								Exit
							EndIf
						EndIf
					Endif
				Endif
			Enddo
			If InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'BEMATECH','BEMA8501','SWEDA','NSWEDA','DARUMA','EPSON')
				Select crTpmMvItnE
				Loop
			Endif
			oFormulario = Thisform.Parentform.oModemEest
			_ItemCupFis = VerSubt(oFormulario,.t.)
			If _ItemcupFis <> m.cItens
				Select crTpmMvItnE
				Loop
			EndIf
		Endif
		Select crTpmMvItnE
		Skip
		ThisForm.ParentForm.ImpressoItem = .t.
	Endif
EndDo

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE inchequeserial
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE inchequeserial
Thisform.Leitor = .F.
Thisform.ReadComm.leitura()
Thisform.LeituraSerial=Thisform.readcomm.campo

Thisform.Cnt_fpagto.Cnt_Serial.get_bco.valid
if Thisform.leitor
	if not empty(xpar.bancos)
		if not fChkCheques(xpar.bancos,xpar.agencias,xpar.contas,xpar.numeros,.f.,.t.,xpar.Dopes)
			replace bancos with '', agencias with '', contas with '', numeros with ''
			Thisform.Cnt_fpagto.Cnt_Serial.get_bco.value = ''
			Thisform.Cnt_fpagto.Cnt_Serial.get_age.value = ''
			Thisform.Cnt_fpagto.Cnt_Serial.get_conta.value = ''
			Thisform.Cnt_fpagto.Cnt_Serial.get_num.value = ''
			Thisform.LeituraSerial = ''
			Thisform.Cnt_fpagto.Cnt_Serial.get_bco.setfocus
		else
			Thisform.Cnt_fpagto.Cnt_Serial.get_num.setfocus	
		endif
	endif
else
	Thisform.Cnt_fpagto.Cnt_Serial.get_age.setfocus
endif
Thisform.leitor = .F.
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE iniciarcupom
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE iniciarcupom
If InList(crSigCdOpe.ImpClis,[O])
	cgc = ThisForm.Cnt_Identifica.Destino.Get_cpf.Value
	Cgc = Strt( Strt( Strt( Cgc, ".", "" ), "-", "" ), "/", "" )
	If Empty(CGC)
		MessageBox('Obrigatório Informar uma Conta com CPF/CNPJ.',0+64,'')
		Return .f.
	Endif
Endif

If Not ThisForm.VerStatus()  &&Checar Status da Impressora
	ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Impfiscal.Visible = .T.
	Return .f.
Endif

ThisForm.ParentForm.Checkstat = .f.

*** Tiago - 21/12/2010 - Comentado pq a função ainda não existe, mas não esquecer de descomentar
*!*	If CrSigOpCdd.PafEcfs = 1 And Not FchkMd5(CrSigCdOpe.Dopes,ThisForm.PodataMgr)
*!*		MessageBox('Parâmetros de Função Incorreto!!!',0+16,'')
*!*		Return .f.
*!*	EndIf

lnOrdem = 0
lcSql = [Select * From SigFiNtb Where Imps=']+CrSigFiFis.Imps+[' And Ordems = ]+Str(CrSigCdOpe.TrNfis,2)
If ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcsql,'CrSigFiNtb') > 0
	lnOrdem = crSigFiNtb.OperNF2
Endif
Select crSigFiFis
Goto top
Do Case
	Case InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'BEMATECH','BEMA8501','DARUMA','S/IMPRESSORA','EPSON')
		If crSigCdOpe.CupFis=1
			ThisForm.Inicia=ThisForm.AbreCupom(oFormulario)
		Else
			ThisForm.Inicia = .t.
		Endif
		ThisForm.parentform.AbriuCupom = ThisForm.Inicia

	Case  InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'SWEDA','NSWEDA','ELGIN')
		Do Case
			Case crSigCdOpe.CupFis=1
				ThisForm.Inicia=ThisForm.AbreCupom(oFormulario)
				ThisForm.parentform.AbriuCupom = ThisForm.Inicia
			Case crSigCdOpe.CupFis=3
				ThisForm.Inicia=AbrirCupomNF(oFormulario,lnOrdem)
				ThisForm.parentform.AbriuCupom = ThisForm.Inicia
			OtherWise
				Messagebox('Operação não é Cupom Fiscal !!',0+48,'')
		EndCase
	Case  InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'DATAREGIS','NDATAREGIS')
		If crSigCdOpe.CupFis=1
			ThisForm.Inicia=ThisForm.AbreCupom(oFormulario)
		Else
			ThisForm.Inicia = .t.
		Endif
EndCase

Return .f.

ENDPROC

PROCEDURE itemfiscal
Local llRetorno

If CrSigCdOpe.CupFis = 1 And crSigCdOpe.digitens = 1 And crSigFiFis.Imps <> 'BEMATECH'
	ThisForm.SubTotac = .t.
Endif

llRetorno = Thisform.ParentForm.ItemFiscal()
Return (llRetorno)

ENDPROC

	.get_agendc.value=space(1)
	.get_conta.value=space(10)
	.get_contadc.value=space(1)
	.get_num.value=space(6)
	.get_numdc.value=space(6)
	.get_Pca.Value = Space(34)

PROCEDURE mexcluiitem
Local lnRecNo

Select crTpmMvItn
_cIt = cItens
Delete

Thisform.CItem = Thisform.CItem - 1

Thisform.cntItem.Get_Total.Value = Thisform.Parentform.TotIte

Select crTpmMvItnC

	Delete While cItens = _cIt
EndIf

Thisform.GrdCupom.Refresh

Select crTpmMvItn
Goto Bottom

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE mgetfpag
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE mgetfpag
LcQuery = [Select a.Fpags, a.Descrs, a.MoeFpgs, a.Fparcs, 0 as Obrigs, a.cCupomNvs, a.Situas, a.nCodFinzs, ]+;
		  [a.nFpags, a.Trocos, a.Infos, a.Acumulas, a.libcsens, o.autos, a.ChkBins, a.BlqTabDs From SigOpFp a, SigOpFpo o ]+;
		  [Where a.Fpags = o.Fpags And a.nCodFinzs > 0 and o.dopes = ']+TprMvCab.Dopes+[']
If Thisform.Parentform.PoDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'CrTmpFpag') < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Ok = .f.
EndIf
Select CrTmpFpag

If Reccount() = 0
	LcQuery = [Select Fpags, Descrs, MoeFpgs, Fparcs, 0 as Obrigs, cCupomNvs, Situas, nCodFinzs, nFpags, ]+;
			  [Trocos, Infos, Acumulas, libcsens, 0 as autos, chkbins, BlqTabDs From SigOpFp Where nCodFinzs > 0]
	If Thisform.Parentform.PoDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'CrTmpFpag') < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
		Ok = .f.
	EndIf
	vCond = Iif(Inlist(CrSigCdOpe.Copers, 1, 2), 'Inlist(Situas, "A", " ", "' + ;
			Iif(CrSigCdOpe.Copers=1, 'R', 'P') + '")', '.t.')
	Select Fpags, Descrs, MoeFpgs, Fparcs, Obrigs, nFpags, Trocos, Infos, Acumulas, libcsens, Autos, ChkBins, BlqTabDs From CrTmpFpag ;
		Where &vCond. Into Cursor ccSigOpFp ReadWrite
Else
	Select * From CrTmpFpag Into Cursor ccSigOpFp ReadWrite
EndIf
Select ccSigOpFp
Index On nFpags Tag nFpags
Index On Fpags Tag Fpags

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE mlimpacntitem
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE mlimpacntitem
LParameters plInsere

If plInsere
	Select crTpmMvItn
	Go Top
	Delete For Empty(Cpros)
	If Not Seek( Spac( 14 ), 'crTpmMvItn', 'Cpros' )
		Select [ ] as Agrupar, Max(Citens) as Citens From crTpmMvItn Where cpros <> ' ' Into Cursor ItenEesti group by 1
		Select ItenEesti
		Go top
		ThisForm.Citem = Iif(ThisForm.UltimoCancel>itenEesti.cItens,ThisForm.UltimoCancel,itenEesti.cItens) + 1
		Insert Into crTpmMvItn(emps,dopes,numes,citens ) ;
			Value (TprMvCab.emps,TprMvCab.dopes,TprMvCab.numes, Thisform.citem)
	EndIf
	Select crTpmMvItn
	Set Order to 
EndIf

With Thisform.CntItem
	.Cnt_Produto.Get_Produto.Value = ''
	.Cnt_Produto.Get_Qtds.Value = 0
	.Cnt_subTotal.Get_Valor.Value = 0
	.Cnt_Desconto.Get_UniVal.Value = 0
	.Cnt_Desconto.Get_ValDesc.Value = 0
	.Cnt_Desconto.Get_PValDesc.Value = 0
	.Cnt_Desconto.Get_ValAcre.Value = 0
	.Cnt_Desconto.Get_PValAcre.Value = 0
	.Cnt_produto.Get_Produto.Refresh
EndWith
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE montatela
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE montatela
lnTop = 102
With ThisForm.CntItem
	If .Cnt_Desconto.Visible
		.Cnt_Desconto.Top = lnTop
		lnTop = .Cnt_Desconto.top + .Cnt_Desconto.Height + 2
	EndIf
	If .Cnt_SubTotal.Visible
		.Cnt_SubTotal.Top = lnTop
		lnTop = .Cnt_SubTotal.Top + .Cnt_SubTotal.Height + 2
	EndIf
EndWith

With ThisForm.Cnt_identifica
	ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.identifica.Enabled = .f.
	lnTop = 41
	.CodOperacao.Visible  = InList(crSigCdOpe.Numeras,3,4) Or InList(CrSigCdOpe.PedClis,1,3)

	.TabDesconto.Get_listaPreco.Visible = (CrSigOpCdc.HablPrecs = 0 And InList( crSigCdOpe.LPrecos, 1, 2, 4, 5 ) )
	.TabDesconto.Say_ListaPreco.visible = (CrSigOpCdc.HablPrecs = 0 And InList( crSigCdOpe.LPrecos, 1, 2, 4, 5 ) )

	If InList( crSigCdOpe.tabdescs, 1, 2, 4, 5 ) Or ( crSigCdOpe.Precops = 1 And ;
			InList( crSigCdOpe.LPrecos, 1, 2, 4, 5 ) And .TabDesconto.Get_ListaPreco.Visible )
		.TabDesconto.Visible = .t.
		ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.identifica.Enabled = .t.
	Else
		.TabDesconto.visible = .f.
	EndIf
	.Origem.Visible  = ( crSigCdOpe.Origems = 1 And Not CrSigCdOpe.FixGOrigs And Not CrSigCdOpe.FixCOrigs )
	.Destino.Visible = ( crSigCdOpe.Destinos = 1 )
	.Vendedor.Visible = InList(crSigCdOpe.Vendes, 1, 2, 4, 5 )
	.Responsavel.Visible = InList(crSigCdOpe.ComisDivs, 1,3,4,5 )
	
	* Botões de Acesso ao Cadastro de Clientes
	With ThisForm.Cnt_Identifica.Origem
		llCli = (crSigCdOpe.CadClis = 1 And Not crSigCdOpe.CadCliAuts = 1 )
		.btnCadastros.Visible  = llCli
	EndWith
	With ThisForm.Cnt_Identifica.Destino
		llCli = (crSigCdOpe.CadClis = 2 And Not crSigCdOpe.CadCliAuts = 1 )
		.btnCadastros.Visible  = llCli
	EndWith
	* Fim // Botões de Acesso ao Cadastro de Clientes

	If .CodOperacao.Visible
		lnTop = .CodOperacao.Top + .CodOperacao.Height + 2
		ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.identifica.Enabled = .t.
	EndIf
	If .TabDesconto.Visible
		.TabDesconto.Top = lnTop
		lnTop = .TabDesconto.Top + .TabDesconto.Height + 2
		ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.identifica.Enabled = .t.
	EndIf
	If .Origem.Visible
		.Origem.Top = lnTop
		lnTop = .Origem.Top + .Origem.Height + 2
		ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.identifica.Enabled = .t.
	Endif
	If .Destino.Visible
		.Destino.Top = lnTop
		lnTop = .Destino.Top + .Destino.Height + 2
		ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.identifica.Enabled = .t.
	Endif
	If .Vendedor.Visible
		.Vendedor.Top = lnTop
		lnTop = .Vendedor.Top + .Vendedor.Height + 2
		ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.identifica.Enabled = .t.
	Endif
	If .Responsavel.Visible
		.Responsavel.Top = lnTop
		lnTop = .responsavel.Top + .Responsavel.Height + 2
		ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.identifica.Enabled = .t.
	EndIf
	ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Identifica.Visible  = ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Identifica.Enabled
	
EndWith

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE mostraobs
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE mostraobs
Lparameters lNewFpg

Local Info, FPag

FPag = xPar.FPags
=ThisForm.parentform.podatamgr.cursorquery('SigOpFp','CrSigOpFp','Fpags',xPar.Fpags)
Info = crSigOpFp.Infos

With ThisForm.Cnt_fpagto
	.GetObs.Visible = ( crSigOpFp.Obs = 1 )
	.SayObs.Visible = ( crSigOpFp.Obs = 1 )
	.get_obs.Visible=.F.
	.say9.Visible=.F.
	*!*.Cnt_ChequepreTef.Visible = .f.
	.Cnt_ChequepreTef.cnt_chq.visible = .f.

	If Not lNewFpg

		Select xPar
		If (CrSigOpFp.EmiVales = 3) Or (crSigCdOpe.DigObs = 1)
			With .Get_Obs
				.InputMask = Iif( crSigOpFp.EmiVales = 3 And Not Empty(AllTrim(crSigOpFp.cPictures)), ;
							 crSigOpFp.cPictures, '' )
				.MaxLength = Iif( crSigOpFp.EmiVales = 3 And Not Empty(AllTrim(crSigOpFp.cPictures)), ;
							 Len(AllTrim(crSigOpFp.cPictures)), 20 )
				.Width = .MaxLength * 7 + 10 + 2 * .Margin
				.Visible = .T.
				.Refresh
			EndWith
			.say9.Visible=.T.
		EndIf
		
		If info='C' And CrSigCdOpe.InibcChqs # 1
			Select crSigCdPam
			goto top
			.Cnt_ChequepreTef.Visible = .t.
			.Cnt_ChequepreTef.cnt_chq.visible = .T.
			Cgc = Strt( Strt( Strt(.Cnt_ChequepreTef.cnt_chq.Get_Cpf.Value, ".", "" ), "-", "" ), "/", "" )
			If Empty(Cgc)
				Cgc = Strt( Strt( Strt(ThisForm.Cnt_identifica.Destino.Get_Cpf.Value, ".", "" ), "-", "" ), "/", "" )
			Endif
			.Cnt_ChequepreTef.cnt_chq.Get_Cpf.Value = cgc
			.Cnt_ChequepreTef.cnt_chq.opc_cpf.Value = 2

			If Len(Alltrim(Cgc))<>14
				.Cnt_ChequepreTef.cnt_chq.opc_cpf.Value = 1
			EndIf
			If Not InList(CrSigCdPam.pdvcchqs,1,2)
				.Cnt_ChequepreTef.cnt_chq.TefChq.Caption = 'Valida C\<heque'
			Endif
			.Cnt_ChequepreTef.refresh
		EndIf
		
	Endif
	This.Refresh
EndWith
Select xPar
IF !EOF() and !BOF()
	GO RECNO()
ENDIF

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE mvalidavalor
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE mvalidavalor
Local lnValor, lnValorUnitario, lnQtde, lnValorTotal

With Thisform.CntItem
	lnValor = .Cnt_Subtotal.Get_Valor.Value
	lnValorUnitario = .Cnt_Desconto.Get_UniVal.Value
	lnQtde = .Cnt_Produto.Get_Qtds.Value
	lnValorTotal = lnValorUnitario * lnQtde
EndWith

If lnValor # lnValorTotal
	MessageBox('Valor Total Informado Está Incorreto.', 48, ;
			   'Quantidade x Valor Unitário # Valor Informado' )
	Return( .F. )
Else
	Return( .T. )
EndIf

ENDPROC

* PROCEDURE total_item
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE total_item
Lparameters pTotaliza

Select crTpmMvItn
_cit = crTpmMvItn.Citens
=ThisForm.ParentForm.Calc_ValIte()
Thisform.ParentForm.TotIte = fTotalizarItn(ThisForm.parentform.Podatamgr)
If ptotaliza && PAF - Tiago - 11/03/2011 - Atualizava sempre o total
	Thisform.CntItem.Get_Total.Value = Thisform.ParentForm.TotIte
EndIf
Thisform.parentform.Atuatot()
With ThisForm.Mensagems
	Select crTpmMvItn
	Locate for cItens = _cit
	xTexto1 = ''
	xTexto2 = ''
	If not Empty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros)
		xTexto1 = Padl(crTpmMvItn.Cpros,14,'0') +' '+crTpmMvItn.Dpros
		xTexto2 = Transform(crTpmMvItn.Qtds,"@Z 999.99")+' '+crTpmMvItn.cunis+' X '+;
				  Transform(crTpmMvItn.Units,"@Z 999,999.99")+' = '+;
				  Transform(crTpmMvItn.Totas,"@Z 999,999.99")
	EndIf
	.mensage1.Caption = xTexto1
	.mensage2.Caption = xTexto2
	.Refresh
EndWith

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE valida24hs
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE valida24hs
Lparameters pDataImp, pSeriFab

If ThisForm.ParentForm.Libera24hs
	Return .t.
Endif

lcDtEcf = Ttoc(pDataImp)
pDiaAnt = ( Ttod(pDataImp) - 1 )
lcHora  = Substr(lcDtEcf,12,2)
pDtMcr  = fDtoSQL(Date(),'23:55:00')

If ( Ttod(pDtMcr) = Ttod(pDataImp) And pDataImp > pDtMcr ) Or InList(lcHora,'00','01')

	lcSql = [Select Top 1 Serifab,dtredzs From SigFiRx Where SeriFab = ']+pSeriFab+[' Order by DtRedZs Desc ]
	If ThisForm.PoDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'LocalRelx') < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão (Valida24Hs - LocalRelx)')
		Return 0
	EndIf
	
	Select LocalRelx
	Go Top
	
	lLiberado = .f.
	
	If InList(lcHora,'00','01') And Ttod(LocalRelx.DtRedzs) = pDiaAnt
		lLiberado = .t.
	Else
		llData = LocalRelx.DtRedZs
		lcSql = [Select a.Nemps, Max(a.Datas) as Datas From SigMvCab a, SigCdOpe b ]+;
				[Where a.Datas > ?llData And a.dopes = b.dopes and b.cupfis in (1,3) ]+;
				[And a.nemps = ']+pSeriFab+[' group by a.nemps ]

		If ThisForm.PoDataMgr.Sqlexecute(lcSql,'MovEest') < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão (Valida24Hs - MovEest)')
			Return 0
		EndIf
		
		Select MovEest
		If Reccount('MovEest') > 0
			MessageBox('Obrigatório Realização da Redução Z da ECF.',0+64,'Atenção!!!')
		Else
			lLiberado = .t.
		Endif
	EndIf

	ThisForm.ParentForm.Libera24hs = lLiberado

	Return (lliberado)
Else
	Return .t.
EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE validacertficado
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE validacertficado
Lparameters pSta, pCnx
Local lcRet, llFec, ldDat, lnErr, lcQry
lcRet = []
llFec = .f.
If (Type([pSta])=[C]) And Not Empty(pSta) 
	ldDat = pCnx.GetDatetime()
	lnDia = Dow(ldDat)
	lnErr = pCnx.SqlExecute([Select * From SigCdSts Where Cods = '] + Padr(pSta, 3) + ['], [crAr1])
	If (lnErr < 1)
		=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!! Falha na Conexão (ValidaCertificado)], 0+16, [Erro!!! ])
		Return []
	Else
		Go Top In crAr1
		If Not Eof([crAr1])
			lcStr = crAr1.ddVals
			If (Substr(lcStr, lnDia, 1) = [0]) And lcStr <> '00000000  '
				lcRet = [A Movimentação Está Bloqueada ] 
				Do Case
					Case (lnDia = 1)
						lcRet = lcRet + [aos Domingos!!!] 
					Case (lnDia = 2)
						lcRet = lcRet + [nas Segundas-feiras!!!] 
					Case (lnDia = 3)
						lcRet = lcRet + [nas Terças-feiras!!!] 
					Case (lnDia = 4)
						lcRet = lcRet + [nas Quartas-feiras!!!] 
					Case (lnDia = 5)
						lcRet = lcRet + [nas Quintas-feiras!!!] 
					Case (lnDia = 6)
						lcRet = lcRet + [nas Sextas-feiras!!!] 
					Case (lnDia = 7)
						lcRet = lcRet + [nos Sábados!!!] 
				EndCase
			Else
				If (lcStr = [00000000  ])
					lcRet = [A Movimentação Está Bloqueada Para Uso!!!] 
				Endif
			EndIf
			If Empty(lcRet) And (Substr(lcStr, 8, 1) = [0])
				If fChkFeriado(pCnx, ldDat)
					lcRet = [A Movimentação Está Bloqueada em Feriados!!!] 
				EndIf
			EndIf
		EndIf
	EndIf
EndIf
If Used([crAr1])
	Use In crAr1
EndIf
Return lcRet
************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE verifsubtotal
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE verifsubtotal
Local llOK, llError1, lcOnError1

If ThisForm.Subtotac And Not InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'S/IMPRESSORA')
	Clear Typeahead
	Keyboard '{BACKSPACE}' 
	m.llError1 = .f.
	m.lcOnError1 = On('Error')
	On Error m.llError1 = .t.
	llOk = Inkey(.5, 'MH')
	On Error &lcOnError1.
	If ThisForm.CancelCupom()
		llOk = 7
		DO While llOk = 7
			llOk = MessageBox('O Total do Cupom não Confere Com Total de Itens do Sistema. '+Chr(13)+;
						'Por Favor Tente outra Vez ou Cancele o Cupom!!!'+Chr(13)+;
						'Aperte SIM para continuar o sistema',4+16+256,'Atenção..')
		EndDo
		Return .f.
	EndIf
EndIf
ThisForm.SubTotAc = .f.
Return .t.

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE verstatus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE verstatus
Local llRet

lcCaption = Thisform.cnt_mensagem.menSAGEM.Caption

lcNoCaixa  = ''
lcNoInterv = ''

oFormulario = Thisform.Parentform.oModemEest
llRet = .f.

If CrSigCdOpe.CupFis = 1 And Not ThisForm.Parentform.CheckStat
	llRet = .t.
Else
	Thisform.cnt_mensagem.menSAGEM.Caption = 'Verificando status!!!'
	DO Case
		Case InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'BEMATECH','BEMA8501','EPSON')
			VerStatus = RotStatus(' ',oFormulario)
			If Type('VerStatus')<>'L' And Not Empty(VerStatus)
				llRet = .t.
			EndIf
		
		Case InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'DARUMA')
			VerStatus = RotStatus('NOK',oFormulario)
			If Type('VerStatus')<>'L' And Not Empty(VerStatus)
				llRet = .t.
			EndIf

		Case InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'DATAREGIS','NDATAREGIS')
			VerStatus = RotStatus(3,oFormulario)
			If Type('VerStatus')<>'L' And Substr(VerStatus,2,1) = 'S'
				llRet = .t.
			EndIf
			
		Case InList(CrSigFiFis.Imps,'SWEDA','NSWEDA','ELGIN')
			VerStatus = RotStatus('NOK',oFormulario)
			If Type('VerStatus')<>'L' And Not Empty(VerStatus)
				llRet = .t.
			EndIf

		Case InList(CrSigFiFis.Imps,'S/IMPRESSORA')
			llRet = .t.

	EndCase
EndIf

If llRet
	Thisform.cnt_mensagem.menSAGEM.Caption = 'Verificando No Fabricação!!!'
	lnNoFabr = LeituraFab(oFormulario)
	If Type('lnNoFabr')='C'
		If !Empty(lnNoFabr)
			lcImpf = fDecriptografar(alltrim(lnNoFabr))
			lcWhere = [Select * From SigFiMpf Where cimpfabs = ']+lcImpf+[' And Emps=']+ThisForm.parentform.pEmps+[']
			LnErro = ThisForm.parentForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWhere,'CrSigFiMpf')
			If LnErro < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão (CrSigFiMpf 2)')
				llRet = .f.
			Else
				Select CrSigFiMpf
				If Reccount() = 0
					MessageBox('Impressora Fiscal Inválida para esta Loja !!',0+48,'ERRO')
					llRet = .f.
				EndIf
				If CrSigFiMpf.ImpAtivas
					MessageBox('Impressora Fiscal Inativa!!',0+48,'ERRO')
					llRet = .f.
				Endif
			EndIf
			If llRet
				ThisForm.parentform.pAbreGaveta = crSigFiMpf.Gavetas
				ThisForm.parentform.NoCaixa = crSigFiMpf.cnCaixas
				ThisForm.parentform.pimpcheque = CrSigFiMpf.Impches
				ThisForm.ParentForm.TemAutentica = ( CrSigFiMpf.Autenticas <> 2 )

				lcNoCaixa = crSigFiMpf.cnCaixas
				lcNoInterv = crSigFiMpf.cnIntervs

				If ThisForm.ParentForm.pAbreGaveta
					DO Case
						Case  InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'DATAREGIS','NDATAREGIS')
							VerStatus = RotStatus(.t.,oFormulario)
							If Type('VerStatus')<>'L' And Substr(VerStatus,4,1) = 'S'
								DO Form SigOpGvt With CrSigFiFis.Imps,oFormulario
							Endif
						Case InList(CrSigFiFis.Imps,'SWEDA','NSWEDA')
							VerStatus = StatGaveta(oFormulario)
							If Type('VerStatus')<>'L' And VerStatus = '0'
								DO Form SigOpGvt With CrSigFiFis.Imps,oFormulario
							Endif
						Case InList(CrSigFiFis.Imps,'BEMATECH','BEMA8501','DARUMA')
							VerStatus = StatGaveta(oFormulario)
							If Type('VerStatus')<>'L' And VerStatus = '00'
								DO Form SigOpGvt With CrSigFiFis.Imps,oFormulario
							Endif
						Case InList(CrSigFiFis.Imps,'EPSON')
							VerStatus = StatGaveta(oFormulario)
							If Type('VerStatus')<>'L' And VerStatus = 0
								DO Form SigOpGvt With CrSigFiFis.Imps,oFormulario
							Endif
						Case CrSigFiFis.Imps = 'ELGIN'
							VerStatus = StatGaveta(oFormulario)
							If Type('VerStatus')<>'L' And VerStatus = '0'
								DO Form SigOpGvt With CrSigFiFis.Imps,oFormulario
							Endif
					EndCase
				Endif

				If InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'DATAREGIS','NDATAREGIS') And Not ThisForm.Parentform.CheckStat
					*** Checar somente a primeira vez devido a demora de 5 segundos
				Else
					Thisform.cnt_mensagem.menSAGEM.Caption = 'Verificando No Caixa!!!'
					lcnCaixa = LeCaixa(oFormulario)
					If Empty(lcnCaixa)
						Messagebox('Não foi possível ler o No do Caixa da Impressora Fiscal, Tente Novamente !!',0+48,'')
						llRet = .f.
					Else
						If Not IsEmpty(CrSigOpCdc.CnCaixas) And CrSigOpCdc.CnCaixas <> lcnCaixa
							MessageBox('O Numero do Caixa Cadastrado na Operação está diferente da ECF'+Chr(13)+;
										'OPERAÇÃO:'+CrSigOpCdc.CnCaixas+'  ECF :'+lcnCaixa,0+16,'')
							llRet = .f.
						EndIf
						If ThisForm.parentform.NoCaixa <> lcnCaixa
							MessageBox('O Numero do Caixa do Cadastro de Impressora Fiscal'+Chr(13)+;
										'Nao confere com o No. do Caixa da ECF !!!'+Chr(13)+;
										'CADASTRO:'+ThisForm.ParentForm.NoCaixa+'  ECF :'+lcnCaixa,0+16,'')
							llRet = .f.
						Endif
					Endif
				EndIf
			Endif
		Else
			Messagebox('Não foi possível ler o No de Fabricação da Impressora Fiscal, Tente Novamente !!',0+48,'')
			llRet = .f.
		Endif
	Else
		Messagebox('Não foi possível ler o No de Fabricação da Impressora Fiscal, Tente Novamente !!',0+48,'')
		llRet = .f.
	Endif
	If llRet
		llDataPadrao = Datetime() &&ThisForm.ParentForm.PodataMgr.GetDatetime()
		Thisform.cnt_mensagem.menSAGEM.Caption = 'Verificando Data/Hora !!!'
		lcDataImp = LeituraDataHora(oFormulario)
		If !IsEmpty(lcDataImp)
			If Abs( ( llDataPadrao - Ctot(lcDataImp) ) / 60 ) > 15
				Messagebox('Hora do Micro Difere em Quinze Minutos da Hora da Impressora !!'+chr(13)+;
						'Micro : '+ttoc(llDataPadrao)+Chr(13)+;
						'  ECF : '+ttoc(Ctot(lcDataImp))+Chr(13),16,'ERRO')
				llRet = .f.
			Endif
			If Ttod(llDataPadrao) # Ttod(Ctot(lcDataImp))
				Messagebox('Data do Micro Difere da Data da Impressora !!'+chr(13)+;
						'Micro : '+ttoc(llDataPadrao)+Chr(13)+;
						'  ECF : '+ttoc(Ctot(lcDataImp))+Chr(13),16,'ERRO')
				llRet = .f.
			EndIf
		Else
			Messagebox('Erro na Leitura da Data da Impressora Fiscal, Tente novamente !!',0+48,'')
			llRet = .f.
		Endif
	EndIf

	If llRet And Not ThisForm.Valida24hs(Ctot(lcDataImp),lnNoFabr )
		llRet = .f.
	Endif

	Thisform.cnt_mensagem.menSAGEM.Caption = ' '
	If llRet
		llRet = ThisForm.parentform.Valida_Bemat()
	EndIf
	
	If llRet And InList(CrSigFiFis.Imps,'DATAREGIS','NDATAREGIS','DARUMA','BEMATECH','BEMA8501','S/IMPRESSORA','NSWEDA','EPSON') ;
				And CrSigCdPam.CodOpers = 1 And goSistema.Operador = '000'
		DO Form SIGOPPRR
		If goSistema.Operador = '000'
			MessageBox('Código do Operador Não Cadastrado!!!',0+48,'Obrigatório Operador')
			llRet = .f.
		Endif
	Endif

	If llRet
		Replace TprMvCab.NEmps with lnNoFabr ;
			    TprMvCab.ecfs  with lcNoCaixa+lcNoInterv In TprMvCab

		=ThisForm.Podatamgr.Cursorquery('SigCdUsu','CrUsu','Usuarios',goSistema.Operador,[nComps])
		ThisForm.Caption = AllTrim( TprMvCab.Dopes ) + '     [ Documento : '+Str(TprMvCab.Numes,6)+'] '+Space(40)+'ECF: '+Iif(Used('crSigFiMpf'),crSigFiMpf.cnCaixas,'')+Space(20)+'Operador(a) : '+goSistema.Operador+' '+Alltrim(crUsu.nComps)
	Endif
EndIf

Thisform.cnt_mensagem.menSAGEM.Caption = lcCaption

Return (llRet)
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Init
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Init
LParameters pForm, pnDatSes

Local lnLeft, llOk, lnDeslocTop
Public _OldCodG

_oldCodG = 0
Set DataSession to pnDatSes
pForm.Enabled = .F.

Select crSigFiFis
Goto Top
If CrSigFiFis.Imps = 'S/IMPRESSORA'
	Thisform.CaixaLivre.CaixaLivre.Caption = 'CAIXA LIVRE'
	Thisform.CaixaLivre.CaixaLivre2.Caption = Thisform.CaixaLivre.CaixaLivre.Caption
EndIf

With Thisform
	.Top = 0
	.Left = 0
	.Enabled = .t.
	.OldCodigo = 0
	.Inicia = .f.
	.ParentForm = pForm
	.Op_Escolha = pForm.pcEscolha
	.nControle = 0
	.PodataMgr = .ParentForm.PodataMgr
	=ThisForm.Podatamgr.Cursorquery('SigCdUsu','CrUsu','Usuarios',goSistema.Operador,[nComps])
	.TxtCaption.Caption = '  '+AllTrim( TprMvCab.Dopes ) + '     [ No : '+Str(TprMvCab.Numes,6)+'] '+Space(15)+'CAIXA: '+;
				Iif(Used('crSigFiMpf'),crSigFiMpf.cnCaixas,'')+Space(15)+'Operador(a) : '+goSistema.Operador+' '+;
				Alltrim(crUsu.nComps)
	.LockScreen = .T.
	.ValidOk = .F.
	.TotalGeral = 0
	.lAltControle = .F.
	.lAlteraValor = .F.
	.lAlteraPreco = fChecaAcesso( PadL( CrSigCdOpe.nDopes, 8, '0' ), "ALTPRECO" ) And ;
				    InList( Thisform.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) And ;
				    ( CrSigCdOpe.EPrecos # 1 )
	.CaixaLivre.Visible = .t.
	.Barra_Botoes.MudaOpe.Visible = .f.
	.EditaOrigem = .t.
	.EditaDestino = .t.
	.ConsultaExecutada = .f.
	.retchpsa = 0
	.SenhaAutoIns = .f.
	.Leituranova = .f.
	.CpfCheque   = Space(20)
	.Validado = .t.
	.validadesconto = .f.
	.parentform.validadesconto = ( CrSigCdOpe.Vars <> 3 )
	.QtdValid = 0
	.LibAcumula = .f.
	.Newchv = 1
	.Buscaproduto = .F.
	.Produto = Space(14)
	.LiberaPca = .f. And Not InList(CrSigCdPam.pdvCchqs,1,2)
	.peantvalue = 0
	.Libtroco = .f.
	.Cancelouitem = .f.
	.SubTotAc = .f.
	.TotChamada = 0
	.UltimoCancel = 0
	.UsuLibs = ' '
	.Bcds = 0
	.Pcbandeira = ''
	.pcRede = ''
	.GerouNCupom = .f.
	.Refresh
	.Zorder
EndWith

ThisForm.Cnt_Mensagem.abertura.Caption = []

lcQuery = [Select Seqs,cNivels From SigMvCxs where cDopes = ']+CrSigCdOpe.Dopes+[' Order by Seqs,cNivels ]
If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'LocalSqOpe') < 1)
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (LocalSQOpe)')
	Return .f.
EndIf
ThisForm.CmbTOpers.Clear
Select LocalSqOpe
If Reccount() > 0
	Select LocalSqOpe
	nItem = 1
	Scan 
		If fAcessoMovmto(Usuar, LocalSqOpe.cNivels)
			ThisForm.cmbTOpers.AddItem(Padl(LocalSqOpe.Seqs, 1, '0') + [-] + LocalSqOpe.cNivels)
			nItem = nItem + 1
			If nItem = 9
				Exit
			EndIf
		Endif
	EndScan
	ThisForm.CmbTOpers.AddItem(Padl(9,1,'0') + [-] + Padr('Sair',20))
	ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.MudaOpe.Visible = ThisForm.CmbTopers.ListCount > 1
Endif

ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Cnt_ChequepreTef.cnt_chq.get_pca.ReadOnly = ThisForm.LiberaPca

If Not fLerSettings('CrSigSyCom',ThisForm.Podatamgr)
	Return 0
EndIf
Select * From crSigSyCom Where Tipos = 'Leitora Cheques' Into Cursor CrCfcomItc
Select CrCfComItc
Go Top

Create Cursor Pdvlog (Detalhes c(50))

Create Cursor xParTef (Fpags c(12), nParcs n(2), Vencs d, Valos n(12,2), EmpDopNums c(29), Utilizado L, ;
			InsFpags L, Creditos L, ValDescs n(12,2))
Index On EmpDopNums+Fpags Tag fpags

lcSql = [Select Logos From SigCdPac ]
If Thisform.ParentForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'TmpLogo') < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return 0
Endif

lcSql = [Select * From SigOpFpP ]
If Thisform.ParentForm.PodataMgr.Sqlexecute(lcSql,'ItemCFpagP') < 1
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (ItemCFpagP)')
	Return .f.
EndIf

With Thisform.Cnt_Pendencia.GradeMultiSubN
	.RecordSource          = 'TmpOperacao'
	.Codigos.ControlSource = 'TmpOperacao.Codigos'
	.Notas.ControlSource   = 'TmpOperacao.notas'
	.Valor.ControlSource   = 'TmpOperacao.Valos'
EndWith

With Thisform.cnt_credito.gradeDevol
	.RecordSource          = 'TmpDevol'
	.Column1.ControlSource = 'TmpDevol.Codigos'
	.Column2.ControlSource = 'TmpDevol.Dopes'
	.Column3.ControlSource = 'TmpDevol.Numes'
EndWith

With Thisform.CntFinanc.Cnt_GrdBarra.Grade
	.RecordSource          = 'TmpBarFin'

		.Visible = ( CrSigCdOpe.SubNObrigs # 1 ) And ( CrSigCdOpe.DigItens = 1 )

		llOk =  ( CrSigCdOpe.LPrecos = 5 ) And .f. And (CrSigCdOpe.OpCrs = 4)
		Thisform.lControle = llOk
	Else
		.Visible = .F.
		.Enabled = .F.
	EndIf
	.Cnt_produto.Get_qtds.InputMask = lcMask

	.Column1.text1.FontName = 'Courier New'
	.Column1.text1.ForeColor = 16711680
	.Column1.text1.Readonly = .t.
	.Column1.text1.Width = 345
	.SetAll('DynamicForecolor','(Iif(crTpmMvItnc.Cores=0,Rgb(0,0,255),Iif(crTpmMvItnC.Cores=1,Rgb(0,0,160),Iif(crTpmMvItnC.Cores=2,Rgb(0,0,64),Iif(crTpmMvItnC.Cores=3,Rgb(64,0,64),Rgb(255,0,0))))))','Column')
EndWith

 		.Visible = InList( Thisform.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) And fChecaAcesso( padl(CrSigCdOpe.nDopes,8,'0'), "CANCITEM" )
		.Enabled = InList( Thisform.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) And Thisform.ParentForm.CancelaItem

	With .CntItem.Cnt_Desconto

		.Get_PValDesc.Visible = ( CrSigCdOpe.chkDVal = 1 ) And ( CrSigCdOpe.OpCrs # 4 )

	Select TprMvCab
	lcQuery = [Select Iclis, Rclis, Cpfs From SigCdCli Where Iclis = ']+TprMvCab.Contaos+[']
	If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTmpCli') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpCli)')
		Return 0
	EndIf
	.origem.get_dconta.value = CrTmpcli.rclis
	.origem.get_cpf.value = CrTmpcli.cpfs

	lcQuery = [Select Iclis, Rclis, Cpfs From SigCdCli Where Iclis = ']+TprMvCab.ContaDs+[']
	If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTmpCli') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpCli 2)')
		Return 0
	EndIf
	.destino.get_dconta.value = crtmpcli.rclis
	.destino.get_cpf.value = crtmpcli.cpfs

	If InList( crSigCdOpe.Vendes, 1, 2 )
		lcQuery = [Select Iclis, Rclis, Cpfs From SigCdCli Where Iclis = ']+TprMvCab.Vends+[']
		If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTmpCli') < 1)
			=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpCli 3)')
			Return 0
		EndIf
		.vendedor.get_dconta.value = CrTmpcli.rclis
		.vendedor.get_cpf.value = CrTmpcli.cpfs
	Else
		.vendedor.get_dconta.value = ''
		.vendedor.get_cpf.value    = ''
	Endif

	If InList( crSigCdOpe.ComisDivs, 1, 3, 4, 5 )
		lcQuery = [Select Iclis, Rclis, Cpfs From SigCdCli Where Iclis = ']+TprMvCab.Resps+[']
		If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTmpCli') < 1)
			=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpCli 4)')
			Return 0
		EndIf
		.Responsavel.Get_DConta.Value = CrTmpCli.Rclis
		.Responsavel.Get_Cpf.Value = CrTmpCli.Cpfs
	Else
		.Responsavel.Get_DConta.Value = ''
		.Responsavel.Get_Cpf.Value = ''
	Endif

	If !IsEmpty(crSigOpCdc.MascPedCls)
		.CodOperacao.Get_PedCli.InputMask = Alltrim(CrSigOpCdc.MascPedCls)
		.CodOperacao.Get_PedCli.Width = 20 + ( Len(Alltrim(CrSigOpCdc.MascPedCls)) * 7 )
	Endif

EndWith

ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.pendencia.Visible = InList( crSigCdOpe.SubNs, 1, 3, 4 )
ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.CancPagto.Enabled = .F.
ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.CancPagto.visible = .F.
ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Apaga.Enabled = .T.
Thisform.Barra_Botoes.Alteraqtd.Visible = .T.
ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Alteraqtd.Enabled = .T.
ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Altpreço.Enabled = .T.
Thisform.Barra_Botoes.Desconto.Visible = .f.
Thisform.Barra_Botoes.Acrescimo.Visible = .f.
ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Impfiscal.Visible = ( CrSigFiFis.Imps <> 'S/IMPRESSORA' )
ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.pagto.Visible = .t.
ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Financia.Visible  = ( CrSigOpCdd.RcFinas = 1 And Not InList(crSigCdOpe.SubNs,1,3,4) And CrSigCdOpe.CupFis = 3 )

ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.desconto.get_PorcVariação.Value = 0
ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.desconto.get_TotVariação.Value	= 0
ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Acrescimo.get_PorcVariação.Value = 0
ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Acrescimo.get_TotVariação.Value	= 0

ThisForm.Total_item()

Thisform.MontaTela()

ThisForm.lcFoco = ''
With ThisForm.Cnt_Identifica
	.Refresh
	DO Case
		Case .CodOperacao.Visible And InList( crSigCdOpe.CupFis, 1,3 ) And InList(crSigCdOpe.Numeras,3,4)
			ThisForm.lcFoco = 'ThisForm.Cnt_Identifica.CodOperacao.Get_Codigo.SetFocus'

		Case .CodOperacao.Visible And InList( crSigCdOpe.CupFis, 1,3 ) And InList(crSigCdOpe.PedClis,1,3)
			ThisForm.lcFoco = 'ThisForm.Cnt_Identifica.CodOperacao.Get_PedCli.SetFocus'

		Case .TabDesconto.Visible And InList( crSigCdOpe.tabdescs, 1, 2, 4, 5 ) And Empty(.TabDesconto.Get_tabd.Value)
			ThisForm.lcFoco = 'ThisForm.Cnt_Identifica.TabDesconto.Get_Tabd.SetFocus'

		Case .TabDesconto.Visible And crSigCdOpe.Precops = 1 And InList( crSigCdOpe.LPrecos, 1, 2, 4, 5 ) And ;
				Empty(.TabDesconto.Get_ListaPreco.Value) And .TabDesconto.Get_ListaPreco.Visible
			ThisForm.lcFoco = 'ThisForm.Cnt_Identifica.TabDesconto.Get_ListaPreco.SetFocus'

		Case .Origem.Visible And Empty(.Origem.Get_Conta.Value)
			Thisform.lcfoco = 'ThisForm.Cnt_identifica.Origem.get_conta.Setfocus'
			
		Case .Destino.Visible And Empty(.Destino.Get_Conta.Value)
			ThisForm.lcfoco = 'ThisForm.Cnt_identifica.Destino.get_conta.Setfocus'

		Case .Vendedor.Visible And Empty(.Vendedor.Get_Conta.Value) And CrSigCdOpe.ObrigVds # 1
			ThisForm.lcfoco = 'ThisForm.Cnt_identifica.Vendedor.get_conta.Setfocus'
		
		Case .Responsavel.Visible And Empty(.Responsavel.Get_conta.Value) And CrSigCdOpe.ObrigVd2s # 1
			ThisForm.lcfoco = 'ThisForm.Cnt_identifica.Responsavel.get_conta.Setfocus'
	EndCase
EndWith

ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Get_Fpg.Value = ' '
ThisForm.Cnt_FPagto.Get_Tvariacao.Value = 0

ThisForm.LiberaIdent = .f.
If InList(crSigCdOpe.ImpClis,[S],[O]) And Not (crSigCdOpe.SubnObrigs = 1)
	ThisForm.lcfoco = ' '
	ThisForm.LiberaIdent = .t.
	If ThisForm.CntItem.Visible
		ThisForm.Cnt_mensagem.Mensagem.Caption = '[F2] - '+Iif(CrSigFiFis.Imps = 'S/IMPRESSORA','Abrir Operação','Abrir Cupom Fiscal')
	Else
		If CrSigCdOpe.CupFis = 3
			ThisForm.Cnt_mensagem.Mensagem.Caption = '[F11] - Abrir '+Iif(CrSigFiFis.Imps = 'S/IMPRESSORA','Operação','Cupom Não Fiscal')
		Else
			ThisForm.Cnt_mensagem.Mensagem.Caption = '[F11] - Abrir '+Iif(CrSigFiFis.Imps = 'S/IMPRESSORA','Operação','Cupom Fiscal')
		Endif
	Endif
	ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Identifica.Click()
Else
	If ThisForm.CntItem.Visible
		ThisForm.CntItem.Cnt_Produto.Get_Produto.Enabled = .f.
		ThisForm.CntItem.Proximo.Setfocus
		ThisForm.Cnt_mensagem.Mensagem.Caption = '[F2] - Abrir '+Iif(CrSigFiFis.Imps = 'S/IMPRESSORA','Operação','Cupom Fiscal')
	Else
		ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Altpreço.Visible  = .f.
		ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Get_TotForma.Value = 0
		ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Get_totLiquido.Value = 0
		ThisForm.Cnt_FPagto.Get_Tvariacao.Value = 0
		ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Get_Saldo.Value = 0
		ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Visible = .t.
		ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Get_Fpg.Enabled = .f.
		ThisForm.Cnt_fpagto.Proximo.Setfocus
		If CrSigCdOpe.CupFis = 3
			ThisForm.Cnt_mensagem.Mensagem.Caption = '[F11] - Abrir '+Iif(CrSigFiFis.Imps = 'S/IMPRESSORA','Operação','Cupom Não Fiscal')
		Else
			ThisForm.Cnt_mensagem.Mensagem.Caption = '[F11] - Abrir '+Iif(CrSigFiFis.Imps = 'S/IMPRESSORA','Operação','Cupom Fiscal')
		Endif
	EndIf
Endif

thisform.ParentForm.impostoF=0
thisform.ParentForm.impostoE=0
thisform.ParentForm.impostoM=0

Thisform.LockScreen = .F.

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE KeyPress
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE KeyPress
LPARAMETERS nKeyCode, nShiftAltCtrl

LoBotoes = ThisForm.Barra_Botoes
lcItem = ThisForm.CntItem.Cnt_Produto.Get_Produto.Value

If Not ThisForm.Cnt_fpagto.Cnt_ChequepreTef.Visible And Not ThisForm.CancelaItem.Visible
	Do Case
		Case nKeyCode = -2 And ThisForm.Cnt_pendencia.Visible And ThisForm.Cnt_Pendencia.Bot_Pendentes.Enabled && F3
			NoDefault
			ThisForm.Cnt_pendencia.Bot_Pendentes.Click()

		Case nKeyCode = -2 And ThisForm.Cnt_Credito.Visible And ThisForm.Cnt_Credito.Bot_Pendentes.Enabled && F3
			NoDefault
			ThisForm.Cnt_Credito.Bot_Pendentes.Click()

		*** Tiago - 09/08/2013 - Alteração do crediário de carne
		Case nKeyCode = -1 And ThisForm.CntFinanc.Visible
			NoDefault
			ThisForm.CntFinanc.Cnt_GrdBarra.Inserir.Click()

		Case nKeyCode = -3 And ThisForm.CntFinanc.Visible
			NoDefault
			ThisForm.CntFinanc.Cnt_GrdBarra.Excluir.Click()

		Case nKeyCode = -4 And ThisForm.CntFinanc.Visible
			NoDefault
			ThisForm.CntFinanc.Cnt_GrdBarra.Ok.Click()
		***			

		Case nKeyCode = 137 And loBotoes.MudaOpe.visible And Empty(lcItem)&& ALT+F11
			NoDefault
			loBotoes.MudaOpe.Click()

		Case nKeyCode = 16 And Empty(lcItem)&& CTRL+P
			NoDefault
			loBotoes.VerPreco.Click()
		
		Case nKeyCode = -5 And loBotoes.Identifica.visible And ;
					Empty(lcItem) And Not ThisForm.Inicia And ThisForm.LiberaIdent && F6
			NoDefault
			loBotoes.Identifica.Click()
		
	Otherwise
			If ThisForm.validado
				Do Case

					Case nKeyCode = -1 And loBotoes.Itens.Visible And Empty(lcItem) && F2
						NoDefault
						ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Impfiscal.Visible = .f.
						loBotoes.Itens.Click()

					Case nKeyCode = -2 And ThisForm.CntItem.Visible And ;
							loBotoes.Alteraqtd.visible And ;
								ThisForm.CntItem.cnt_Produto.Get_Produto.Enabled And Empty(lcItem)&& F3
						NoDefault
						loBotoes.Alteraqtd.Click()

					Case nKeyCode = -3 And ThisForm.CntItem.Visible And ;
							loBotoes.Apaga.Visible And loBotoes.Apaga.Enabled And ;
								Not ThisForm.lAlteraqtd And Empty(lcItem) And ThisForm.Inicia && F4
						NoDefault
						loBotoes.Apaga.Click()

					Case nKeyCode = -4 And ThisForm.CntItem.Visible And ;
							loBotoes.AltPreço.Visible && F5
						NoDefault
						loBotoes.AltPreço.Click()


					Case nKeyCode = 133 And loBotoes.Pagto.visible And Empty(lcItem)&& F11
						NoDefault
						loBotoes.Pagto.Click()

					Case nKeyCode = -3 And ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Visible And ;
							loBotoes.CancPagto.Visible && F4
						NoDefault
						loBotoes.CancPagto.Click()

					Case nKeyCode = -2 And ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Visible And ;
						ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Get_valinic.Value <> 0 And loBotoes.Desconto.Visible && F3
						NoDefault
						loBotoes.Desconto.Click()

					Case INLIST(nKeyCode,32,96) And ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Visible And ;
						ThisForm.Cnt_Fpagto.Get_valinic.Value <> 0 And loBotoes.Acrescimo.Visible && CTRL + F3
						NoDefault
						loBotoes.Acrescimo.Click()

					Case nKeyCode = -5 And loBotoes.Identifica.visible And ;
							Empty(lcItem) And ThisForm.Inicia && F6
						NoDefault
						ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Impfiscal.Visible = .f.
						loBotoes.Identifica.Click()

					Case nKeyCode = -6 And loBotoes.Credito.Visible And ;
							Empty(lcItem) And ThisForm.Inicia && F7
						NoDefault
						ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Impfiscal.Visible = .f.
						loBotoes.credito.Click()

					Case nKeyCode = -7 And loBotoes.Pendencia.Visible And ;
							Empty(lcItem) And ThisForm.Inicia && F8
						NoDefault
						ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Impfiscal.Visible = .f.
						loBotoes.Pendencia.Click()

					Case nKeyCode = -7 And loBotoes.Financia.Visible And ;
							Empty(lcItem) And ThisForm.Inicia && F8
						NoDefault
						ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Impfiscal.Visible = .f.
						loBotoes.Financia.Click()						

					Case nKeyCode = -2 And ThisForm.Cnt_pendencia.Visible And ;
							ThisForm.Cnt_Pendencia.Bot_Pendentes.Enabled && F3
						NoDefault
						ThisForm.Cnt_pendencia.Bot_Pendentes.Click()

					Case nKeyCode = -2 And ThisForm.Cnt_Credito.Visible And ;
							ThisForm.cnt_Credito.Bot_Pendentes.Enabled && F3
						NoDefault
						ThisForm.Cnt_Credito.Bot_Pendentes.Click()

					Case nKeyCode = -8 And loBotoes.ImpFiscal.Visible And Empty(lcItem)&& F9
						NoDefault
						loBotoes.Impfiscal.Click()
				EndCase
			EndIf
	EndCase
EndIf

ENDPROC

=fConfigGeral()
ENDPROC

PROCEDURE LostFocus
With ThisForm.Cnt_Consulta
	.Cnt_Produto.Get_Produto.Value = ' '
	.Cnt_Produto.Get_Produto.Visible = .f.
	.Cnt_Resultado.Mensage1.Visible = .f.
	.Cnt_Resultado.Mensage2.Visible = .f.
	.Cnt_Resultado.Visible = .f.
	.Cnt_Produto.visible = .f.
	.Get_Unit.Visible = .f.
EndWith
	
lcSql = [Select Logos From SigCdPac ]
If Thisform.ParentForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'TmpLogo') < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return 0
Endif
lcArquivo3 = Sys(2023)+'\'+Alltrim(Sys(2015))+'.jpg'
Clear Resources
With ThisForm.FigJpg
	If Not Isempty(TmpLogo.logos)
		Copy Memo TmpLogo.Logos to (lcArquivo3)
		.Picture = (lcArquivo3)
		.Visible = .T.
	Else
		.Visible = .f.
	Endif
EndWith
ThisForm.Cnt_Consulta.Visible = .f.
ThisForm.Refresh
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
Set Confirm Off

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local lcod,lfan,codcor,xobj,oper,precop,cuni,moec,moev,pcus,pven,cmoe,;
		fcusto,fvenda,llProducao, lcArquivo3, lnFator

If !Empty(This.Value)
	Go Top in CrSigCdPam
	oper   = CrSigCdOpe.Opers
	precop = CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs
	
	Select * From crTpmMvItn Where 0=1 Into Cursor crTpmMvItnPro ReadWrite

	Select crTpmMvItnPro
	Append Blank

	SNumero = fVerificarBarras( This.Value )

	If snumero
		ck=.t.
		Set decimals to 0
		lcSql = [Select cBars,Contas,Empos,Cpros,Qtds,Pesos,codCors,codTams From SigOpEtq Where Cbars = ]+Str(Val(This.Value),14)
		If ThisForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcSql,'CrTmpEti') < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return 0
		Endif
		
		If Reccount('CrTmpEti') > 0 
			CPro = CrTmpEti.Cpros
			This.Value = Cpro
			ck=.f.
			lcQuePro = [Cpros,Dpros,Cunis,Situas,fVendas,fCustos,Pesoms,Moepcs,Moepvs,Pvens,Pcuss,Valors,CodFinP,]+;
						[FigJpgs,Cbars,Moevs,Moecs,Moedas,cGrus,Linhas,Colecoes,mUltComps,vUltComps,mercs,cftios,sgrus]

			lcSql = [Select Cpros,Dpros,Cunis,Situas,fVendas,fCustos,Pesoms,Moepcs,Moepvs,Pvens,Pcuss,]+;
					[Valors,FigJpgs,Cbars,Moevs,Moecs,Moedas,cGrus,Linhas,Colecoes,mUltComps,vUltComps,]+;
					[mercs,cftios,sgrus,CodFinP ]+;
					[From SigCdPro Where Cpros = ']+Cpro+[']
			If ThisForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcSql,'CrTmpPro') < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return 0
			EndIf
		Endif
		Set decimals to 2
		If ck
			lcQuePro = [Cpros,Dpros,Cunis,Situas,fVendas,fCustos,Pesoms,Moepcs,Moepvs,Pvens,Pcuss,Valors,FigJpgs,Cbars,Moevs,Moecs,Moedas,cGrus,Linhas,Colecoes]
			lcSql = [Select Cpros, Cbars From SigCdPro Where Cbars = ]+Str(Val(This.Value),14)
			If Thisform.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'LocalPro') < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return 0
			EndIf
			If Reccount('LocalPro') = 0
				lcProduto = Space(14)
				DO form SigOpBus With .t. TO lcProduto
				This.Value = lcProduto
				If Empty(This.Value)
					Return 0
				Endif
			Endif
			lcSql = [Select Cpros,Dpros,Cunis,Situas,fVendas,fCustos,Pesoms,Moepcs,Moepvs,Pvens,Pcuss,Valors,CodFinP,]+;
					[FigJpgs,Cbars,Moevs,Moecs,Moedas,cGrus,Linhas,Colecoes,mUltComps,vUltComps,mercs,cftios,sgrus ]+;
					[From SigCdPro Where Cbars = ]+Str(Val(This.Value),14)

			If Thisform.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'CrTmpPro') < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return 0
			EndIf

			If Reccount('CrTmpPro') = 0
				This.Value = ' '
				Return 0
			EndIf
		EndIf
	Else
		cpro=This.Value
		lcQuePro = [Cpros,Dpros,Cunis,Situas,fVendas,fCustos,Pesoms,Moepcs,Moepvs,Pvens,Pcuss,Valors,FigJpgs,CodFinP,]+;
					[Cbars,Moevs,Moecs,Moedas,cGrus,Linhas,Colecoes,Ean13,mUltComps,vUltComps,mercs,cftios,sgrus,custofs ]
		If fVerificarBarras(Cpro,9)
			lcSql = [Select Cpros,Dpros,Cunis,Situas,fVendas,fCustos,Pesoms,Moepcs,Moepvs,Pvens,Pcuss,cftios,sgrus,custofs,]+;
					[Valors,FigJpgs,Cbars,Moevs,Moecs,Moedas,cGrus,Linhas,Colecoes,Ean13,mUltComps,vUltComps,mercs,CodFinP ]+;
					[From SigCdPro Where Cbars = ]+Str(Val(This.Value),14)
				
			If Thisform.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'CrTmpPro') < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return 0
			EndIf

			If Reccount('CrTmpPro') = 0
				lcSql = [Select Cpros,Ean13 From SigCdPro Where Ean13 = ]+Str(Val(Cpro),13)
				If ThisForm.Parentform.PodataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CrTmpPro') < 1
					MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
					Return 0
				EndIf
			Endif
		Else
			Cpro = Padr(Cpro,14)
		EndIf
		lcSql = [Select Cpros From SigCdPro Where Cpros = ']+cPro+[']
		If Thisform.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'LocalPro') < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return 0
		EndIf
		If Reccount('LocalPro') = 0
			lcProduto = Space(14)
			DO form SigOpBus With .f.,CrSigCdOpe.ItSituas TO lcProduto
			This.Value = lcProduto
			Cpro = lcProduto
			If Empty(This.Value)
				Return 0
			Endif
		Endif
		
		If Not ThisForm.Parentform.podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdPro','CrTmpPro','Cpros',cpro,lcQuepro)
			This.Value = ' '
			Return 0
		Endif
	Endif
	This.Value = CrTmpPro.Cpros
	This.Refresh
	
	Select crTpmMvItnPro
	replace Cpros     With CrTmpPro.Cpros,;
			CodBarras With CrTmpPro.Cbars,;
			Dpros     With CrTmpPro.Dpros,;
			CUnis     With CrTmpPro.Cunis,;
			Qtds      With 1 in crTpmMvItnPro

	ThisForm.Cnt_Consulta.Cnt_Resultado.Mensage1.Caption = Transform(crTpmMvItnPro.CodBarras,"@L 99999999999999")+' - '+Alltrim(crTpmMvItnPro.Cpros)
	ThisForm.Cnt_Consulta.Cnt_Resultado.Mensage2.Caption = Alltrim(crTpmMvItnPro.Dpros)

	If CrSigCdOpe.Imagems = 1
		lcArquivo3 = Sys(2023)+'\'+Alltrim(Sys(2015))+'.jpg'
		If Not Empty(crTmpPro.FigJpgs) And Not IsNull(crTmpPro.FigJpgs)
			Clear Resources
			Copy Memo CrTmpPro.FigJpgs to (lcArquivo3)
			With ThisForm.FigJpg
				.Picture = (lcArquivo3)
				.Visible = .T.
			EndWith
		Else
			Clear Resources
			With ThisForm.FigJpg
				If Not Isempty(TmpLogo.logos)
					Copy Memo TmpLogo.Logos to (lcArquivo3)
					.Picture = (lcArquivo3)
					.Visible = .T.
				Else
					.Visible = .f.
				Endif
			EndWith
		EndIf
	Endif

	=ThisForm.parentform.podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdUni','CrTmpUni','Cunis',CrTmpPro.Cunis)
	=Thisform.parentform.podatamgr.Cursorquery('SigCdGpr','CrTmpGgrp','codigos',CrTmpPro.Mercs)

	Select crTpmMvItnPro
	Replace crTpmMvItnPro.MoeFats With Iif( CrSigCdOpe.Precops = 1, CrTmpPro.Moepvs, Iif( CrSigCdOpe.Precops = 2, ;
									CrTmpPro.Moepcs, CrSigCdOpe.CMoes ) ) in crTpmMvItnPro
		
	If InList(CrSigCdOpe.PrecoAuts,1,3)
		lcSql = [Select Codigos from SigCdLpc Where lPrecos = ']+TprMvCab.lPrecos+[']
		lnErro = ThisForm.parentform.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CrTmplPre')
		If lnErro < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return 0
		EndIf
		lcwhere = [Select * From SigCdLpi Where Cpros = ']+CrTmpPro.Cpros+[' And LPrecos = ']+TprMvCab.lPrecos+[']
		lnErro = ThisForm.parentform.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWhere,'CrSigCdLpi')
		If lnErro < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return 0
		EndIf
		Select CrSigCdLpi
		Go Top
		If Reccount() > 0 And (IsEmpty(crSigCdLpi.VencFs) Or BetWeen(TprMvCab.Datas,crSigCdLpi.VencIs, crSigCdLpi.VencFs))
			Replace crTpmMvItnPro.univals   with crSigCdLpi.PVens ,;
					crTpmMvItnPro.lPrecos   With TprMvCab.lPrecos ,;
					crTpmMvItnPro.lPrecos   with TprMvCab.LPrecos,;
					crTpmMvItnPro.CodlPrecs With CrTmplPre.Codigos in crTpmMvItnPro
		Else
			If InList(CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs,8,9)
				=fCustoMedio(TprMvCab.Emps,CrTmpPro.Cpros,TprMvCab.Datas,ThisForm.Podatamgr,(CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs=8))
				lcMoeCus = TmpCusMed.Moedas
				lnVlrCus = TmpCusMed.Valors
			EndIf
			lnpCus = CrTmpPro.PCuss
			If InList(CrSigOpCdd.chkIfors,1,2)
				_Ct = Iif(CrSigOpCdd.ChkIfors=1,TprMvCab.ContaOs,TprMvCab.Contads)
				lcQuery = [Select Moevs, Pvens From SigPrFnc Where Cpros = ']+CrTmpPro.Cpros+[' And Ifors = ']+_Ct+[']
				=ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcQuery,'CrTmpFpro')
				If Not Eof('CrTmpfPro') And CrTmpfPro.pvens > 0 and Not Empty(CrTmpfPro.Moevs)
					lnpCus = crtmpfPro.PVens
				Endif
			Endif
			Replace crTpmMvItnPro.univals with Iif( CrSigCdOpe.Precops = 1, CrTmpPro.PVens, ;
										Iif( CrSigCdOpe.Precops = 2, lnpCus, ;
										Iif( CrSigCdOpe.Precops = 5, CrTmpPro.Valors, ;
										Iif( CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 7, CrTmpPro.vUltComps,;
										Iif( CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 8, lnVlrCus, Iif( CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 11, crTmpPro.custofs, 0 ) ) ) ) ) ) in crTpmMvItnPro
		EndIf
	Endif

	Select crTpmMvItnPro
	If Empty( crTpmMvItnPro.opers )
		Replace crTpmMvItnPro.opers with Iif( oper=1, 'E', Iif( oper = 2, 'S', crTpmMvItnPro.opers ) )
	Endif

	Thisform.ParentForm.Calc_ValIte('crTpmMvItnPro')

	Thisform.Cnt_Consulta.Get_unit.Value = crTpmMvItnPro.Units

EndIf

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGMVITN.Cnt_Consulta.Command1
* Classe: commandbutton (Base: commandbutton)
* Registro: 13
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
With ThisForm.Cnt_Consulta
	.Cnt_Produto.Get_Produto.Value = ' '
	.Cnt_Produto.Get_Produto.Visible = .f.
	.Cnt_Resultado.Mensage1.Visible = .f.
	.Cnt_Resultado.Mensage2.Visible = .f.
	.Cnt_Resultado.Visible = .f.
	.Cnt_Produto.visible = .f.
	.Get_Unit.Visible = .f.
EndWith

lcSql = [Select Logos From SigCdPac ]
If Thisform.ParentForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'TmpLogo') < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return 0
Endif
lcArquivo3 = Sys(2023)+'\'+Alltrim(Sys(2015))+'.jpg'
Clear Resources

With ThisForm.FigJpg

	If Not Isempty(TmpLogo.logos)
		Copy Memo TmpLogo.Logos to (lcArquivo3)
		.Picture = (lcArquivo3)
		.Visible = .T.
	Else
		.Visible = .f.
	Endif
EndWith
ThisForm.Cnt_Consulta.Visible = .f.
Thisform.Refresh
ENDPROC
