================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGMVITN.CntItem.Cnt_Produto.Get_Produto
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 19
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
Set Confirm Off
If Not Empty(This.Value)
	ThisForm.lalteraqtd = .f.
Endif
If Empty(This.Value) And Lastkey() = 13
	ThisForm.CntItem.proximo.SetFocus
EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local lcod,lfan,codcor,xobj,oper,precop,cuni,moec,moev,pcus,pven,cmoe,;
		fcusto,fvenda,llProducao, lcArquivo3, lnFator

Declare laCampo(2,2)
If !Empty(This.Value)
	Go Top in CrSigCdPam
	oper   = CrSigCdOpe.Opers
	precop = CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs
	cpro   = ''
	dpro   = ''
	peso   = 0
	qtd    = 0 

	If Thisform.CntItem.cnt_produto.Get_Qtds.Value = 0
		Thisform.CntItem.cnt_produto.Get_qtds.Value = 1
		ThisForm.Preencheu = .t.
	EndIf
	qtd = Thisform.CntItem.cnt_produto.Get_qtds.Value
	Thisform.CntItem.cnt_produto.Get_qtds.Refresh

	Select crTpmMvItn
	Goto Bottom

	SNumero = fVerificarBarras( This.Value )
	If snumero
		ck=.t.
		Select crTpmMvItn
		_Recnx = Recno()
		_Ordem = Order()
		Set Order to CodBarras
		If Seek( Val( This.Value ) ) And Recno() = _Recnx
			Skip
		Endif

		If !Eof() And CodBarras = Val( This.Value ) And CrSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs <> 4
			Messagebox( 'Código de Barras já digitado nesta Operação...', 48, '' )
			Set Order to &_Ordem
			Go _Recnx
			This.Value = Thisform.antcpro
			With Thisform.CntItem
				.Cnt_Produto.Get_Qtds.Value     = 0
				.Cnt_subtotal.Get_Valor.Value    = 0
				.Cnt_Desconto.Get_UniVal.Value   = 0
				.Cnt_Desconto.Get_ValDesc.Value  = 0
				.Cnt_desconto.Get_PValDesc.Value = 0
			EndWith
			Return 0
		Endif
			
		Set Order to &_Ordem
		Go _Recnx
		Set decimals to 0
		lcSql = [Select Contas,Empos,Cpros,Qtds,Pesos,Cbars, codCors, codTams From SigOpEtq Where Cbars = ]+Str(Val(This.Value),14)
		If ThisForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcSql,'CrTmpEti') < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return 0
		Endif
		
		If Reccount('CrTmpEti') > 0 
			Do Case
				Case Empty( CrTmpEti.Contas ) And CrSigCdOpe.Estoqs = 1 And Val( This.Value ) # crTpmMvItn.CodBarras
					Messagebox( 'Esta etiqueta já foi vendida!', 48, '' )
					This.Value = Thisform.antcpro
					Return 0

				Case CrSigCdOpe.EstOrigs = 2 And TprMvCab.Contaos # CrTmpEti.Contas And CrSigCdOpe.Estoqs = 1 And Val( This.Value ) # crTpmMvItn.CodBarras
					Messagebox( 'Esta etiqueta não pertence a este estoque!', 48, '' )
					This.Value = Thisform.antcpro
					Return 0

				Case CrSigCdOpe.EstDests = 2 And TprMvCab.Contads # CrTmpEti.Contas And CrSigCdOpe.Estoqs = 1 And Val( This.Value ) # crTpmMvItn.CodBarras 
					Messagebox( 'Esta etiqueta não pertence a este estoque!', 48, '' )
					This.Value = Thisform.antcpro
					Return 0

				Case CrSigCdOpe.Transs = 1 And TprMvCab.Emps # CrTmpEti.Empos And CrSigCdOpe.Estoqs = 1 And Val( This.Value ) # crTpmMvItn.CodBarras 
					Messagebox( 'Esta etiqueta não pertence a esta empresa!', 48, '' )
					This.Value = Thisform.antcpro
					Return 0

				Other
					CPro = CrTmpEti.Cpros
					If Empty( crTpmMvItn.qtds )
						qtd=crtmpeti.qtds
					Else
						qtd=crTpmMvItn.qtds
					EndIf
					peso=crtmpeti.pesos
					replace crTpmMvItn.etiesps with .t., ;
							crTpmMvItn.codbarras with val(This.Value) in crTpmMvItn
					This.Value = Cpro
					ck=.f.
					lcQuePro = [Cpros,Dpros,Cunis,Situas,fVendas,fCustos,Pesoms,Moepcs,Moepvs,Pvens,Pcuss,Valors,CodFinP,]+;
								[FigJpgs,Cbars,Moevs,Moecs,Moedas,cGrus,Linhas,Colecoes,mUltComps,vUltComps,mercs,cftios,sgrus]

					lcSql = [Select Cpros,Dpros,Cunis,Situas,fVendas,fCustos,Pesoms,Moepcs,Moepvs,Pvens,Pcuss,CodFinP,]+;
							[Valors,FigJpgs,Cbars,Moevs,Moecs,Moedas,cGrus,Linhas,Colecoes,mUltComps,vUltComps,mercs,cftios,sgrus ]+;
							[From SigCdPro Where Cpros = ']+Cpro+[']
					If ThisForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcSql,'CrTmpPro') < 1
						MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
						Return 0
					Endif
			Endcase
		Endif
		Set decimals to 2
		If ck
			lcQuePro = [Cpros,Dpros,Cunis,Situas,fVendas,fCustos,Pesoms,Moepcs,Moepvs,Pvens,Pcuss,Valors,FigJpgs,Cbars,Moevs,Moecs,Moedas,cGrus,Linhas,Colecoes]
			lcSql = [Select Cpros, Cbars From SigCdPro Where Cbars = ]+Str(Val(This.Value),14)
			If Thisform.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'LocalPro') < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return 0
			EndIf
			If Reccount('LocalPro') = 0
				lcProduto = Space(14)
				DO form SigOpBus With .t. TO lcProduto
				This.Value = lcProduto
				If Empty(This.Value)
					Return 0
				Endif
			Endif
			lcSql = [Select Cpros,Dpros,Cunis,Situas,fVendas,fCustos,Pesoms,Moepcs,Moepvs,Pvens,Pcuss,Valors,CodFinP,]+;
					[FigJpgs,Cbars,Moevs,Moecs,Moedas,cGrus,Linhas,Colecoes,mUltComps,vUltComps,mercs,cftios,sgrus ]+;
					[From SigCdPro Where Cbars = ]+Str(Val(This.Value),14)

			If Thisform.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'CrTmpPro') < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return 0
			EndIf

			If Reccount('CrTmpPro') > 0
				=ThisForm.parentform.podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdUni','CrTmpUni','Cunis',CrTmpPro.Cunis)
				If CrTmpUni.etiqs='S'  And CrSigCdOpe.SemEtiqs # 1 And ( CrSigCdOpe.opers = 2 Or ( CrSigCdOpe.Opers = 3 And crTpmMvItn.opers = 'S' ) )
					messagebox('Código Errado - Etiqueta Especial !!',0+48,'')
					This.Value=''
					this.Refresh
					Thisform.validok=.f.
					return 0
				Else
					If ( CrSigCdOpe.ItSituas = 1 And crtmpPro.Situas = 2 ) Or ;
					   ( CrSigCdOpe.ItSituas = 2 And crtmpPro.Situas # 2 )
						MessageBox('Situação do Produto ' + AllTrim(crtmpPro.Cpros) + ': ' + ;
						    Iif(CrTmpPro.Situas = 2,'Inativo','Ativo') + '.' + Chr(13) + ;
						    'Operação Permite Somente Produtos ' + ;
						    Iif(CrSigCdOpe.ItSituas = 1, 'Ativos', Iif(CrSigCdOpe.ItSituas = 2, 'Inativa', ;
						    'Ambos')) + '.', 48, 'Situação do Produto Incorreta Para a Operação')
						This.Value=''
						this.Refresh
						Thisform.validok=.f.
						return 0
					EndIf
				EndIf
				If Reccount('CrTmpEti') > 0 Or CrSigCdPac.BarIndivs < 2
					Replace crTpmMvItn.etiesps with .t. in crTpmMvItn
				Endif
				cpro=CrTmpPro.cpros
				dpro=CrTmpPro.dpros
				peso=CrTmpPro.pesoms
			Else
				This.Value = ''
				Thisform.validok=.f.
				Return 0
			EndIf
		EndIf
	Else
		cpro=This.Value
		lcQuePro = [Cpros,Dpros,Cunis,Situas,fVendas,fCustos,Pesoms,Moepcs,Moepvs,Pvens,Pcuss,Valors,FigJpgs,CodFinP,]+;
					[Cbars,Moevs,Moecs,Moedas,cGrus,Linhas,Colecoes,Ean13,mUltComps,vUltComps,mercs,cftios,sgrus,custofs ]
		If fVerificarBarras(Cpro,9) And CrSigCdPac.barindivs <> 3
			lcSql = [Select Cpros,Dpros,Cunis,Situas,fVendas,fCustos,Pesoms,Moepcs,Moepvs,Pvens,Pcuss,cftios,sgrus,custofs,]+;
					[Valors,FigJpgs,Cbars,Moevs,Moecs,Moedas,cGrus,Linhas,Colecoes,Ean13,mUltComps,vUltComps,mercs,CodFinP ]+;
					[From SigCdPro Where Cbars = ]+Str(Val(This.Value),14)
				
			If Thisform.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'CrTmpPro') < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return 0
			EndIf

			If Reccount('CrTmpPro') = 0
				lcSql = [Select Cpros,Ean13 From SigCdPro Where Ean13 = ]+Str(Val(Cpro),13)
				If ThisForm.Parentform.PodataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CrTmpPro') < 1
					MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
					Return 0
				EndIf
			Endif
			Select CrTmpPro
			Go Top
			If Reccount() > 0 And CrSigCdPac.BarIndivs < 2
				replace crTpmMvItn.etiesps with .t. in crTpmMvItn
				cpro = CrTmpPro.Cpros
			EndIf
		Else
			Cpro = Padr(Cpro,14)
		EndIf
		lcSql = [Select Cpros From SigCdPro Where Cpros = ']+cPro+[']
		If Thisform.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'LocalPro') < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return 0
		EndIf
		If Reccount('LocalPro') = 0
			lcProduto = Space(14)
			DO form SigOpBus With .f.,CrSigCdOpe.ItSituas TO lcProduto
			This.Value = lcProduto
			Cpro = lcProduto
			If Empty(This.Value)
				Return 0
			EndIf
		Else
			cPro = LocalPro.Cpros
		Endif
		If Not ThisForm.Parentform.podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdPro','CrTmpPro','Cpros',cpro,lcQuepro)
			This.Value = ''
			Thisform.validok=.f.
			Return 0
		Endif
		=ThisForm.parentform.podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdUni','crTmpUni','Cunis',CrTmpPro.Cunis)
		If crTmpUni.etiqs='S'  And CrSigCdOpe.SemEtiqs # 1 And ( CrSigCdOpe.opers = 2 Or ( CrSigCdOpe.Opers = 3 And crTpmMvItn.opers = 'S' ) )
			messagebox('Código Errado - Etiqueta Especial !!',0+48,'')
			This.Value=''
			this.Refresh
			Thisform.validok=.f.
			return 0
		Else
			If ( CrSigCdOpe.ItSituas = 1 And CrTmpPro.Situas = 2 ) Or ;
			   ( CrSigCdOpe.ItSituas = 2 And CrtmpPro.Situas # 2 )
				MessageBox('Situação do Produto ' + AllTrim(CrTmpPro.Cpros) + ': ' + ;
				    Iif(CrTmpPro.Situas = 2,'Inativo','Ativo') + '.' + Chr(13) + ;
				    'Operação Permite Somente Produtos ' + ;
				    Iif(CrSigCdOpe.ItSituas = 1, 'Ativos', Iif(CrSigCdOpe.ItSituas = 2, 'Inativa', ;
				    'Ambos')) + '.', 48, 'Situação do Produto Incorreta Para a Operação')
				This.Value=''
				this.Refresh
				Thisform.validok=.f.
				return 0
			EndIf
		endif
		This.Value = CrTmpPro.CPros
		cpro = CrTmpPro.CPros
		dpro = CrTmpPro.DPros
		cuni = CrTmpPro.CUnis
		peso = CrTmpPro.Pesoms
	Endif
	If ( CrSigCdOpe.LPrecos = 5 )
		If Empty( TprMvCab.LPrecos )
			MessageBox('Lista de Preços Não Informada.', 48, 'Lista de Preços Obrigatória')
			This.Value = ''
			Return 0
		EndIf
		laCampo(1,1)='Cpros'
		laCampo(1,2)= CrTmpPro.Cpros
		laCampo(2,1)='LPrecos'
		laCampo(2,2)= TprMvCab.LPrecos
		If !ThisForm.parentform.podatamgr.ChkRegister('SigCdLpi',@laCampo)
			MessageBox('Produto ' + AllTrim(CrTmpPro.Cpros) + ' Não Pertence a' + Chr(13) + ;
					   'Lista de Preços ' + Proper(AllTrim(TprMvCab.LPrecos)) + '.', 48, ;
					   'Lista de Preços Obrigatória' )
			This.Value = ''
			Return 0
		EndIf
	EndIf
		
	If crSigCdOpe.TabdObrigs = 3

		lcWhere = [Select * From SigOpTdt Where Codigos = ']+TprMvCab.Tabds+[']
		lnErro = ThisForm.Parentform.PodataMgr.SqlExecute(lcWhere,'CrSigOpTdt')
		If lnErro < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão (CrSigOpTdt)')
			This.Value = ''
			Return 0
		EndIf
		Select CrSigOpTdt
		Index On Codigos + Str(Tipos,1) + CodTips Tag CodTips

		If InList(CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs,8,9)
			=fCustoMedio(TprMvCab.Emps,CrTmpPro.Cpros,TprMvCab.Datas,ThisForm.Parentform.PodataMgr,(CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs=8))
		EndIf
		lcMoevs = CrTmpPro.Moecs
		If InList(CrSigOpCdd.chkIfors,1,2)
			_Ct = Iif(CrSigOpCdd.ChkIfors=1,TprMvCab.ContaOs,TprMvCab.Contads)
			lcQuery = [Select Moevs, Pvens From SigPrFnc Where Cpros = ']+CrTmpPro.Cpros+[' And Ifors = ']+_Ct+[']
			=ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcQuery,'CrTmpFpro')
			If Not Eof('CrTmpfPro') And CrTmpfPro.pvens > 0 and Not Empty(CrTmpfPro.Moevs)
				lcMoeVs   = crtmpfPro.MoeVs
			Endif
		Endif

		lcMoedas = Iif( crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 1, PadR( CrTmpPro.MoeVs, 10 ), ;
		           Iif( InList(crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs,2,11), PadR( lcMoevs, 10 ), ;
		           Iif( CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 5, Padr( CrTmpPro.Moedas,10 ), ;
		           Iif( CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 7, Padr( CrTmpPro.mUltComps,10),;
		           Iif( CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 8, Padr( TmpCusMed.Moedas,10), Space(10))))))

		Select crSigOpTdt
		Set Order to CodTips
		If Seek( TprMvCab.Tabds + '4' + CrtmpPro.CPros ) Or ;
				Seek( TprMvCab.Tabds + '9' + CrTmpPro.CodFinP ) Or ;
				Seek( TprMvCab.Tabds + '1' + CrTmpPro.CGrus ) Or ;
				Seek( TprMvCab.Tabds + '2' + CrTmpPro.Linhas ) Or ;
				Seek( TprMvCab.Tabds + '3' + CrTmpPro.Colecoes ) Or ;
				Seek( TprMvCab.TabDs + '5' + lcMoedas ) Or ;
				Seek( TprMvCab.TabDs + '6' + CrTmpPro.Cunis ) Or ;
				Seek( TprMvCab.TabDs + '7' + CrTmpPro.Cftios ) Or ;
				Seek( TprMvCab.TabDs + '8' + CrTmpPro.Sgrus )
		Else
			MessageBox('O Produto '+CrTmpPro.Cpros+' não está configurado na Tabela de Desconto ' + AllTrim(TprMvCab.Tabds),0+48,'')
			This.Value = ' '
			Return 0
		EndIf
	EndIf

	Select crTpmMvItn
	Replace crTpmMvItn.cpros with cpro
	This.Value = Cpro
	This.Refresh

	If CrSigCdOpe.Imagems = 1
		lcArquivo3 = Sys(2023)+'\'+Alltrim(Sys(2015))+'.jpg'
		If Not Empty(crTmpPro.FigJpgs) And Not IsNull(crTmpPro.FigJpgs)
			Clear Resources
			Copy Memo CrTmpPro.FigJpgs to (lcArquivo3)
			With ThisForm.FigJpg
				.Picture = (lcArquivo3)
				.Visible = .T.
			EndWith
		Else
			Clear Resources
			With ThisForm.FigJpg
				If Not Isempty(TmpLogo.logos)
					lcFoto=Strconv(Strtran(Strtran(Strtran(CrTmpPro.FigJpgs,[data:image/png;base64,],[]),[data:image/jpeg;base64,],[]),[data:image/jpg;base64,],[]),14)
					StrToFile(lcFoto, lcArquivo3)
					.Picture = (lcArquivo3)
					.Visible = .T.
				Else
					.Visible = .f.
				Endif
			EndWith
		EndIf
	Endif
	=ThisForm.parentform.podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdUni','CrTmpUni','Cunis',CrTmpPro.Cunis)
	=Thisform.parentform.podatamgr.Cursorquery('SigCdGpr','CrTmpGgrp','codigos',CrTmpPro.Mercs)

	If CrSigCdOpe.PesoAuts = 1
		Replace crTpmMvItn.Pesos with Peso In crTpmMvItn
	Endif
			
	If CrSigCdOpe.FatAuts = 1
		lnFator = Iif( CrSigCdOpe.Precops = 1, CrTmpPro.FVendas, Iif( CrSigCdOpe.Precops = 2, CrTmpPro.FCustos, 0 ) )
		Replace crTpmMvItn.Fators With lnFator in crTpmMvItn
	Endif
		
	Select crTpmMvItn
	Replace crTpmMvItn.MoeFats With Iif( CrSigCdOpe.Precops = 1, CrTmpPro.Moepvs, Iif( CrSigCdOpe.Precops = 2, CrTmpPro.Moepcs, CrSigCdOpe.CMoes ) )
		
	Thisform.CntItem.cnt_produto.Get_qtds.Value = Qtd
		
	If Empty( crTpmMvItn.CodBarras )
		Replace crTpmMvItn.Units With 0 in crTpmMvItn
	Endif
			
	If InList(CrSigCdOpe.PrecoAuts,1,3)
		lcSql = [Select Codigos from SigCdLpc Where lPrecos = ']+TprMvCab.lPrecos+[']
		lnErro = ThisForm.parentform.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CrTmplPre')
		If lnErro < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return 0
		EndIf
		lcwhere = [Select * From SigCdLpi Where Cpros = ']+CrTmpPro.Cpros+[' And LPrecos = ']+TprMvCab.lPrecos+[']
		lnErro = ThisForm.parentform.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWhere,'CrSigCdLpi')
		If lnErro < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return 0
		EndIf
		Select CrSigCdLpi
		Go Top
		If Reccount() > 0 And (IsEmpty(crSigCdLpi.VencFs) Or BetWeen(TprMvCab.Datas,crSigCdLpi.VencIs, crSigCdLpi.VencFs))
			Replace crTpmMvItn.univals   with crSigCdLpi.PVens ,;
					crTpmMvItn.lPrecos   With TprMvCab.lPrecos ,;
					crTpmMvItn.lPrecos   with TprMvCab.LPrecos,;
					crTpmMvItn.CodlPrecs With CrTmplPre.Codigos in crTpmMvItn
		Else
			If InList(CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs,8,9)
				=fCustoMedio(TprMvCab.Emps,CrTmpPro.Cpros,TprMvCab.Datas,ThisForm.Podatamgr,(CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs=8))
				lcMoeCus = TmpCusMed.Moedas
				lnVlrCus = TmpCusMed.Valors
			EndIf
			lnpCus = CrTmpPro.PCuss
			If InList(CrSigOpCdd.chkIfors,1,2)
				_Ct = Iif(CrSigOpCdd.ChkIfors=1,TprMvCab.ContaOs,TprMvCab.Contads)
				lcQuery = [Select Moevs, Pvens From SigPrFnc Where Cpros = ']+CrTmpPro.Cpros+[' And Ifors = ']+_Ct+[']
				=ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcQuery,'CrTmpFpro')
				If Not Eof('CrTmpfPro') And CrTmpfPro.pvens > 0 and Not Empty(CrTmpfPro.Moevs)
					lnpCus = crtmpfPro.PVens
				Endif
			Endif
		
			Replace crTpmMvItn.univals with Iif( CrSigCdOpe.Precops = 1, CrTmpPro.PVens, ;
										Iif( CrSigCdOpe.Precops = 2, lnpCus, ;
										Iif( CrSigCdOpe.Precops = 5, CrTmpPro.Valors, ;
										Iif( CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 7, CrTmpPro.vUltComps,;
										Iif( CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 8, lnVlrCus, Iif( CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 11, crTmpPro.custofs, 0 ) ) ) ) ) ) in crTpmMvItn
		EndIf
	Endif

	Select crTpmMvItn
	Replace crTpmMvItn.dpros with CrTmppro.dpros, ;
			crTpmMvItn.CUnis with CrTmppro.CUnis,;
			crTpmMvItn.Qtds  With Qtd in crTpmMvItn
		
	Select crTpmMvItn
	If Empty( crTpmMvItn.opers )
		Replace crTpmMvItn.opers with Iif( oper=1, 'E', Iif( oper = 2, 'S', crTpmMvItn.opers ) )
	Endif

	Thisform.Calc_ValIte()
	With Thisform.CntItem
		.Cnt_SubTotal.Get_Valor.Value  = crTpmMvItn.Units
		.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value = crTpmMvItn.Units
	EndWith

	Thisform.Total_item()
	
	If Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros)
		With ThisForm.Mensagems
			xTexto1 = PadL(crTpmMvItn.Cpros,14,'0')+' '+crTpmMvItn.Dpros
			xTexto2 = Transform(crTpmMvItn.Qtds,"@Z 999.99")+' '+crTpmMvItn.cunis+' X '+;
					  Transform(crTpmMvItn.Units,"@Z 999,999.99")+' = '+;
					  Transform(crTpmMvItn.Totas,"@Z 999,999.99")
			.mensage1.Caption = xTexto1
			.mensage2.Caption = xTexto2
			.Refresh
		EndWith
	EndIf
	If CrSigCdOpe.CodBars = 1 And CrTmpGgrp.BarRefs <> 1
		Select crTpmMvItn
		Replace EtiEsps With .f.
	EndIf
	
	If ThisForm.lAlteraQtd
		Replace EtiEsps With .t. in crTpmMvItn
	Endif

	*** Mena - 11/09/2013 - monta registro de cor e tamanho para verificar estoque
	SELECT crTpmMvIts
	lcTag = ORDER()
	SET ORDER TO 

	IF snumero			&& verifica se é barra para incluir cor e tamanho
		LOCATE for codbarras = crTmpEti.cbars
		IF not FOUND()
			APPEND BLANK
		endif
		replace codcors with crTmpEti.codcors in crTpmMvIts
		replace codtams with crTmpEti.codtams in crTpmMvIts
		replace qtds with crTpmMvItn.qtds in crTpmMvIts
		replace qtdembs with crTpmMvItn.qtds in crTpmMvIts
		replace aqtdembs with crTpmMvItn.qtds in crTpmMvIts
		replace qtbaixas with crTpmMvItn.qtds in crTpmMvIts
		replace cpros with crTpmMvItn.cpros in crTpmMvIts
		replace citens with crTpmMvItn.citens in crTpmMvIts
		replace codbarras with crTmpEti.cbars in crTpmMvIts
	ENDIF
	*** Mena - 28/05/2013 - Verifica estoque neste momento pois qdo é código de barra, pula o valid da quantidade e assim não avisa falta de estoque ***
	Select crTpmMvItn
	_cIt = crTpmMvItn.Citens
	If !fverest(ThisForm.parentform.podatamgr)
		*** Mena - 14/10/2013 - se não tem estoque suficiente, exclui item para não dar erro de total na impressao do cupom ***	
		Select crTpmMvIts
		Locate For cItens = _cIt
		IF FOUND()
			DELETE
		ENDIF
		ThisForm.lAlteraQtd = .f.
		Select crTpmMvItn
		Locate For cItens = _cIt
		Thisform.mExcluiItem()
		Thisform.mLimpaCntItem( .T. )
		Thisform.Total_item()
		Return 0
 	EndIf
	SELECT crTpmMvIts
	SET ORDER TO TAG (lcTag)
	******************************************************************************

	If crTpmMvItn.EtiEsps And (CrSigCdPac.BarIndivs < 2 Or ThisForm.lAlteraQtd ) And InList( Thisform.Op_escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) 
		If Thisform.citem<CrSigCdOpe.qtdites
			If CrSigCdOpe.Opers # 3 And CrSigCdOpe.EFators # 1 And CrSigCdOpe.EPrecos # 1 And ;
					CrSigCdOpe.EPesos # 1 And CrSigCdOpe.chkDVal # 1 And Not InList(CrSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs,3,4)
				Select crTpmMvItn
				_cIt = crTpmMvItn.Citens
				If fverest(ThisForm.parentform.podatamgr)
					Select crTpmMvItn
					Locate For cItens = _cIt
					If Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_SubTotal.Get_valor.Value<>0
						If Not Thisform.ItemFiscal()
							If Thisform.Cancelcupom()
								Thisform.mExcluiItem()
								ThisForm.Total_item()
							Else
								Select crTpmMvItn
								Replace ImpCupFis with .t.
								ThisForm.Atuacupom()
								ThisForm.Total_Item(.t.) && PAF - Tiago - 11/03/2011								
								ThisForm.ParentForm.CancelaItem = .T.
								ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Apaga.Enabled = .T.
							Endif
						Else
							Select crTpmMvItn
							Replace ImpCupFis with .t.
							ThisForm.Atuacupom()
							ThisForm.ParentForm.CancelaItem = .T.
							ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Apaga.Enabled = .T.
						Endif
					Else
						Messagebox('Valor do Produto Inválido !!',0+48,'')
						Thisform.mExcluiItem()
					Endif
					ThisForm.lAlteraQtd = .f.
					Thisform.mLimpaCntItem( .T. )
					Return 0
				Else
					ThisForm.lAlteraQtd = .f.
					Select crTpmMvItn
					Locate For cItens = _cIt
					Thisform.mExcluiItem()
					Thisform.mLimpaCntItem( .T. )
					Return 0
				Endif
			Endif
		Endif
	Endif
Endif

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Local lRetorno
ThisForm.Preencheu = .f.
Thisform.AntCPro = crTpmMvItn.Cpros
Thisform.AntValu = crTpmMvItn.Units
ThisForm.CntItem.Cnt_produto.cmdenviaprod.Visible = .f.
Set Confirm on
Thisform.Barra_Botoes.Altpreço.Visible = .f.
lRetorno = ( InList( ThisForm.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) And Empty( This.Value ) )
		
Return (lRetorno)
ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGMVITN.CntItem.Cnt_Produto.Get_Qtds
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 20
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
Local llCntrlFrom

If This.Value <= 0
	If Not Empty(Thisform.CntItem.cnt_produto.Get_Produto.Value)
		Messagebox('Quantidade Inválida !!',0+48,'')
	Endif
	Thisform.mExcluiItem()
	Thisform.mLimpaCntItem( .T. )
	Thisform.CntItem.cnt_produto.Get_Produto.SetFocus
Else
	If ThisForm.lAlteraqtd
		ThisForm.CntItem.Cnt_produto.Get_Produto.SetFocus
	Else
		If CrSigCdOpe.EFators # 1 And CrSigCdOpe.EPrecos # 1 And CrSigCdOpe.EPesos # 1 And CrSigCdOpe.chkDVal # 1
			llCntrlFrom = This.Value > 0 And Thisform.lControle And crTpmMvItn.Qtds > 0
			If Thisform.lalteraqtd Or ;
				( Not Thisform.lAlteraPreco Or CrSigCdOpe.EPrecos = 1 Or CrSigCdOpe.ChkDVal = 1 ) And ;
				Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Subtotal.Get_Valor.Value # 0
				Thisform.mLimpaCntItem()
				Thisform.CntItem.cnt_produto.Get_Produto.SetFocus
			Else
				If Lastkey() # -4
					ThisForm.CntItem.Cnt_produto.Cmdenviaprod.Visible = .t.
					ThisForm.CntItem.Cnt_produto.Cmdenviaprod.SetFocus
				Endif
			EndIf
		EndIf
		Thisform.Barra_Botoes.Altpreço.Visible = (ThisForm.lAlteraPreco)
	Endif
EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local RecN, Ord, Totite, Cotacao, Cotcv, ValAU, _SubNiv, CPro

If Lastkey() <> 13
	Return .f.
Endif

If ( CrSigCdOpe.OpCrs = 4 ) And Mod(This.Value, 1) # 0
	MessageBox('Essa Operação Somente Permite Quantidade Inteira.', 48, 'Quantidade Incorreta')
	This.Value = Thisform.AntQtd
	Return 0
EndIf

If This.Value > 0 And Not ThisForm.lAlteraqtd
	Select crTpmMvItn
	_cIt = crTpmMvItn.citens
	If ( CrSigCdOpe.EPesos # 1 ) And crTpmMvItn.EtiEsps And CrSigCdOpe.OpCrs # 4 And Not InList(CrSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs,3,4)
		Replace crTpmMvItn.Qtds With Thisform.AntQtd in crTpmMvItn
		This.Refresh
	Else
		If InList( Thisform.op_escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' )
			cpro=crTpmMvItn.cpros
			lnBr=crTpmMvItn.CodBarras
			lnRg=Recno('crTpmMvItn')
			If crTpmMvItn.EtiEsps And CrSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs = 4 And This.Value <> 0
				lcSql = [Select Qtds, Pesos, aQtds, aPesos From SigOpEtq Where Cbars = ]+Str(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras)
				If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcsql, 'crTmpET') < 1)
					=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpET)')
					Return 0
				EndIf
				Select [] as Agrupar,Sum(Qtds)+This.Value as Qtds From crTpmMvItn where CodBarras = lnBr And ;
					EtiEsps And Recno() <> lnRg ;
				Into Cursor tcrTpmMvItn Group by 1
				lnSaldo = ( CrTmpEt.Qtds - tcrTpmMvItn.Qtds )
				If lnSaldo < 0
					MessageBox('Quantidade não Disponivel!!!',0+16,'')
					This.Value = 0
					This.Refresh
					Return 0
				Endif
			EndIf
			CPro = crTpmMvItn.CPros
			Replace crTpmMvItn.Qtds With This.Value in crTpmMvItn
			Thisform.Total_Item()
			Select crTpmMvItn
			If fVerEst(thisform.parentform.podatamgr,( This.Value = ThisForm.AntQtd ))
				Thisform.AntQtd = This.Value
				Thisform.ParentForm.TotIte = fTotalizarItn(ThisForm.parentform.Podatamgr)
				Thisform.CntItem.Get_Total.Value = Thisform.Parentform.TotIte
				Select crTpmMvItn
				Locate For cItens = _cIt
				If CrSigCdOpe.OpCrs = 4
					Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_SubTotal.Get_Valor.Value = crTpmMvItn.Units * crTpmMvItn.Qtds
				EndIf
			Else
				Select crTpmMvItn
				Locate For cItens = _cIt
				Replace crTpmMvItn.Qtds With 0 in crTpmMvItn
				If CrSigCdOpe.OpCrs = 4
					Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Subtotal.Get_Valor.Value = 0
				EndIf
				This.Value = 0
				This.Refresh
				Return 0
			Endif
		EndIf
	EndIf
	Go Top in CrSigCdPam

	If InList( Thisform.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) 
		If Lastkey()=13 And Thisform.citem<CrSigCdOpe.qtdites
			If CrSigCdOpe.EFators # 1 And CrSigCdOpe.EPrecos # 1 And CrSigCdOpe.EPesos # 1 And CrSigCdOpe.chkDVal # 1
				Select crTpmMvItn
				_cIt = crTpmMvItn.Citens
				If fverest(ThisForm.Parentform.podatamgr,( This.Value = ThisForm.AntQtd ))
					Select crTpmMvItn
					Locate For cItens = _cIt
					Thisform.AntQtd = This.Value
					If ( Not Thisform.lAlteraPreco Or CrSigCdOpe.EPrecos = 1 Or CrSigCdOpe.ChkDVal = 1 )
						If Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Subtotal.Get_Valor.Value # 0
							If !Thisform.ItemFiscal()
								If ThisForm.Cancelcupom()
									Thisform.mExcluiItem()
									ThisForm.Total_item()
								Else
									Select crTpmMvItn
									Replace ImpCupFis with .t.
									ThisForm.Atuacupom()
									ThisForm.ParentForm.CancelaItem = .T.
									ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Apaga.Enabled = .T.
								Endif
							Else
								Select crTpmMvItn
								Replace ImpCupFis with .t.
								ThisForm.Atuacupom()
								ThisForm.ParentForm.CancelaItem = .T.
								ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Apaga.Enabled = .T.
							EndIf
						Else
							Messagebox('Valor do Produto Inválido !!',0+48,'')
							Thisform.mExcluiItem()
						Endif
						Thisform.mLimpaCntItem( .T. )
					EndIf
				Else
					Select crTpmMvItn
					Locate For cItens = _cIt
					Replace crTpmMvItn.qtds with 0 in crTpmMvItn
					This.Value = 0
					This.Refresh
					Return 0
				Endif
			Endif
		Endif
	Endif
Endif

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Local llOk
Thisform.AntQtd = This.Value
Thisform.AntQtd = Iif(ThisForm.Preencheu,0,This.Value)

		CrSigCdOpe.Opcrs = 4 Or InList(CrSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs,3,4)) )

	Keyboard Chr(24)
Endif
Return ( llOk )

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGMVITN.CntItem.Cnt_Subtotal.Get_valor
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 25
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
If InList( Thisform.op_escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) And This.Value > 0
	With Thisform.CntItem
		.Cnt_desconto.Get_ValDesc.Value  = Iif( .Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value > .cnt_subtotal.Get_Valor.Value, .Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value - .Cnt_SubTotal.Get_Valor.Value, 0 )
		.Cnt_desconto.Get_PValDesc.Value = iif( .Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value = 0 Or .Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value <= .cnt_subtotal.Get_Valor.Value , 0, (( .Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value - .Cnt_subtotal.Get_Valor.Value ) / .Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value ) * 100 )
		.Cnt_desconto.Get_PValDesc.Refresh
		.Cnt_desconto.Get_ValAcre.Value  = Abs(Iif( .Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value < .cnt_subtotal.Get_Valor.Value, .Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value - .Cnt_SubTotal.Get_Valor.Value, 0 ))
		.Cnt_desconto.Get_PValAcre.Value = Abs(iif( .Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value = 0 Or .Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value >= .cnt_subtotal.Get_Valor.Value, 0, (( .Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value - .Cnt_subtotal.Get_Valor.Value ) / .Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value ) * 100 ))
		.Cnt_desconto.Get_PValAcre.Refresh		
	EndWith


	iF CrSigCdOpe.chkDVal = 1
		Select crTpmMvItn
		Replace ValDescs With Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_valdesc.Value + (Abs(Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_ValAcre.Value)*-1)
	Endif

	Select crTpmMvItn
	If Not ( ( CrSigCdOpe.OpCrs = 4 ) And .f. )
		Replace Units With This.Value in crTpmMvItn
	EndIf

	If Thisform.lAlteraValor And This.Value # Thisform.nAntValor And Empty( crTpmMvItn.Utilizas )
		Select crTpmMvItn
		Replace crTpmMvItn.Utilizas With 12
	EndIf
		
	Select crTpmMvItn
	_cit = cItens
	Thisform.Total_Item()
	Thisform.ParentForm.TotIte = fTotalizarItn(ThisForm.parentform.Podatamgr)
	Thisform.CntItem.Get_Total.Value = Thisform.ParentForm.TotIte
	Select crTpmMvItn
	Locate for citens = _cIt

	If InList( Thisform.op_escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' )
		If Not Thisform.ItemFiscal()
			If ThisForm.Cancelcupom()
				Thisform.mExcluiItem()
				ThisForm.Total_item()
			Else
				Select crTpmMvItn
				Replace ImpCupFis with .t.
				ThisForm.AtuaCupom()
				ThisForm.ParentForm.CancelaItem = .T.
				ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Apaga.Enabled = .T.
			Endif
		Else
			Select crTpmMvItn
			Replace ImpCupFis with .t.
			ThisForm.AtuaCupom()
			ThisForm.ParentForm.CancelaItem = .T.
			ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Apaga.Enabled = .T.
		Endif
		Thisform.mLimpaCntItem( .T. )
	Endif
Else
	Messagebox('Valor do produto Inválido !!',0+48,'')
	Thisform.mExcluiItem()
	Thisform.mLimpaCntItem( .T. )
	Thisform.MontaTela()
	Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_produto.Get_Produto.SetFocus
Endif

Thisform.lAlteraValor = .F.

Thisform.mLimpaCntItem()
Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_produto.Get_Produto.SetFocus

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local lnValorTotal, lnValorUnitario

IF ThisForm.lAlteraValor And Not ThisForm.ParentForm.Chklimite(This.Value,Thisform.nAntValor,0,0,.t.)	&& mena 22/03/2016 - trocada a ordem dos parametros
	Select crTpmMvItn
	This.value = Thisform.nAntValor
	RETURN 0
EndIf
Select crTpmMvItn
If CrSigCdPam.LimValIts > 0 And This.Value > crSigCdPam.LimValIts
	MessageBox('Valor Informado > Que Valor Limite por Item!!!',0+48,'')
	This.Value = 0
	This.Refresh
	Return 0
EndIf

ENDPROC

* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Thisform.nAntValor = This.Value

Return( InList( Thisform.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) And ;
		Not Empty(Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Produto.Get_Qtds.Value) And ;
		( Thisform.lAlteraValor Or CrSigCdOpe.EPrecos = 1 Or ( CrSigCdOpe.Eprecos # 1 And ;
		  CrSigCdOpe.chkDVal = 1 And Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_UniVal.Value = 0 ) ) )

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGMVITN.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_valdesc
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 28
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
Local llOk, Rec

llOk = .t.

If !(InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'DATAREGIS','NDATAREGIS') And CrSigCdOpe.ChkEDesc = 1 And CrSigCdOpe.chkDVal = 1)
	If InList( Thisform.op_escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) And This.Value => 0 And This.Value < Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value 
		With Thisform.CntItem.cnt_subtotal.Get_valor
			.Value = Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value - This.Value
			.Refresh
			Replace crTpmMvItn.Units With .Value in crTpmMvItn
		EndWith
		IF CrSigCdOpe.ChkEDesc = 1 && PAF - Tiago - 16/03/2011
			thisform.parentform.totcanc = thisform.parentform.totcanc + (This.Value * crTpmMvItn.qtds)
		EndIf	
		With Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_pvaldesc
			.Value = iif(Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value = 0,0,((Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value - Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_SubTotal.Get_valor.Value) / Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value)* 100)
			.Refresh
		EndWith
	Endif
Else
	If InList( Thisform.op_escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) And This.Value => 0 And This.Value < Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value 
		With Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_SubTotal.Get_valor
			.Value = Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value - This.Value
			.Refresh
			Replace crTpmMvItn.Units With .Value in crTpmMvItn
		EndWith
		IF CrSigCdOpe.ChkEDesc = 1 && PAF - Tiago - 16/03/2011
			thisform.parentform.totcanc = thisform.parentform.totcanc + (This.Value * crTpmMvItn.qtds)
		EndIf
		With Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_pvaldesc
			.Value = iif(Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value = 0,0,((Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value - Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Subtotal.Get_valor.Value) / Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value)* 100)
			.Refresh
		EndWith
	Endif
Endif

lnpDesc = Abs(Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_pvaldesc.Value)

If InList( Thisform.op_escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) And This.Value => 0  And This.Value < Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value && PAF - Tiago - 15/03/2011 - Passado para baixo a checagem se altera preço pois não estava enviando o acréscimo para a impressora And CrSigCdOpe.EPrecos # 1
	Select crTpmMvItn
	Goto Bottom

	Replace crTpmMvItn.Units With ThisForm.CntItem.Cnt_Subtotal.Get_Valor.Value in crTpmMvItn
	
	Select crTpmMvItn
	_cIt = crTpmMvItn.citens

	Thisform.Total_Item()
	
	Thisform.ParentForm.TotIte = fTotalizarItn(ThisForm.parentform.Podatamgr)
	Thisform.CntItem.Get_Total.Value = Thisform.ParentForm.TotIte
	
	Select crTpmMvItn
	Locate for citens = _cit

	Select crTpmMvItn
	Replace ValDescs with This.Value , ;
			pDescs   With lnpDesc in crTpmMvItn

	If CrSigCdOpe.EPrecos # 1 && PAF - Tiago - 15/03/2011 - Checagem de altera preço que estava acima foi colocada aqui
		If Not ( Thisform.TemAcrescimo And ValDescs = 0 )
			If !Thisform.ItemFiscal()
				If Thisform.Cancelcupom()
					Thisform.mExcluiItem()
					ThisForm.Total_item()
					llOk = .f.
				Else
					Select crTpmMvItn
					Replace ImpCupFis with .t.
					ThisForm.AtuaCupom()
					ThisForm.ParentForm.CancelaItem = .T.
					ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Apaga.Enabled = .T.
				EndIf
			Else
				Select crTpmMvItn
				Replace ImpCupFis with .t.
				ThisForm.AtuaCupom()
				ThisForm.ParentForm.CancelaItem = .T.
				ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Apaga.Enabled = .T.
			Endif
			
			Thisform.mLimpaCntItem( .T. )
			Thisform.CntItem.cnt_produto.Get_Produto.SetFocus
		EndIf
	Endif
Endif

If (This.Value >= Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value And This.Value # 0) or This.Value < 0 
	llOk = .F.
	Messagebox('Valor ou Percentual Inválido !!',0+48,'')
	Thisform.mExcluiItem()
	Thisform.mLimpaCntItem( .T. )
	Thisform.CntItem.cnt_produto.Get_Produto.SetFocus
Endif

If ( CrSigCdOpe.EPrecos # 1 ) And ( Not llOk Or Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value # 0 )
	If Not ( Thisform.TemAcrescimo And ValDescs = 0 )
		Thisform.mLimpaCntItem()
		Thisform.CntItem.cnt_produto.Get_Produto.SetFocus
	Endif
Endif
ENDPROC

PROCEDURE Valid
If Lastkey() <> 13
	Return .f.
Endif
If This.Value < 0
	This.Value = 0
	Return 0

If This.Value > 0 And This.Value # ThisForm.AntValue
	nAntValor = Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value - This.Value
	IF Not ThisForm.ParentForm.Chklimite(crTpmMvItn.Units,nAntValor,0,0,.t.) 	&& mena - 22/03/2016 foi trocada a ordem dos parametros
		Select crTpmMvItn

		RETURN 0
	EndIf

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGMVITN.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_pvaldesc
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 29
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
Thisform.Barra_Botoes.Altpreço.Visible = .f.

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local lnValor
lnKey = Lastkey()
If Not ( lnKey = 13 Or lnKey = -2 Or lnKey = -4 )
	Return .f.
Endif
If This.Value < 0 && PAF - Tiago - 15/03/2011 - Não pode ter valor negativo
	This.Value = 0
	Return .F.
EndIf

If !(InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'DATAREGIS','NDATAREGIS') And CrSigCdOpe.ChkEDesc = 1 And CrSigCdOpe.chkDVal = 1)
	If Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value > 0
		If This.Value > 0
			lnValor = Round((Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value * This.Value) /100,2 )
			nAntValor = Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value - lnValor
			IF Not ThisForm.ParentForm.Chklimite(crTpmMvItn.Units,nAntValor,0,0,.t.)	&& mena 22/03/2016 - trocada a posicao dos valores nAntValor,crTpmMvItn.Units
				Select crTpmMvItn
				This.value = 0
				RETURN 0
			EndIf
			With Thisform.CntItem.cnt_desconto.Get_valdesc
				.Value = lnValor
				.Refresh
			EndWith
			With Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_SubTotal.Get_valor
				.Value = Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value - Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_desconto.Get_valdesc.Value
				.Refresh
				Replace crTpmMvItn.Units With .Value in crTpmMvItn
			EndWith
		Endif
	Endif
Else
	If Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value > 0
		If This.Value > 0
			lnValor   = (Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value * This.Value) /100
			nAntValor = (Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value - lnValor )
			IF Not ThisForm.ParentForm.Chklimite(crTpmMvItn.Units,nAntValor,0,0,.t.)	&& mena 03/05/2017 - trocada a posicao dos valores nAntValor,crTpmMvItn.Units
				Select crTpmMvItn
				This.value = 0
				RETURN 0
			EndIf
			With Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_valdesc
				.Value = lnValor
				.Refresh
			EndWith
			With Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_SubTotal.Get_valor
				.Value = Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value - Thisform.CntItem.cnt_desconto.Get_valdesc.Value
				.Refresh
				Replace crTpmMvItn.Units With .Value in crTpmMvItn
			EndWith
		Endif
	Endif
Endif
ENDPROC

PROCEDURE LostFocus
Local llOk, Rec

llOk = .t.

With ThisForm.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto
	If !(InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'DATAREGIS','NDATAREGIS') And CrSigCdOpe.ChkEDesc = 1 And CrSigCdOpe.chkDVal = 1)
		If This.Value => 0 And This.Value < .Get_Unival.Value 
			With Thisform.CntItem.cnt_subtotal.Get_valor
				.Value = ThisForm.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value + This.Value
				.Refresh
				Replace crTpmMvItn.Units With .Value in crTpmMvItn
			EndWith
			With .Get_pvalAcre
				.Value = iif(ThisForm.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value = 0,0,((Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_SubTotal.Get_valor.Value - ThisForm.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value ) / ThisForm.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value)* 100)
				.Refresh
			EndWith
		Endif
	Else
		If This.Value => 0 And This.Value < .Get_Unival.Value 
			With Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_SubTotal.Get_valor
				.Value = ThisForm.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value - This.Value
				.Refresh
				Replace crTpmMvItn.Units With .Value in crTpmMvItn
			EndWith
			With .Get_pvalaCre
				.Value = iif(ThisForm.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value = 0,0,((Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Subtotal.Get_valor.Value - ThisForm.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value ) / ThisForm.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value)* 100)
				.Refresh
			EndWith
		Endif
	Endif
	lnpDesc = Abs(.Get_pvalaCre.Value)

	If This.Value => 0  And This.Value < .Get_Unival.Value && PAF - Tiago - 15/03/2011 - Passado para baixo a checagem se altera preço pois não estava enviando o acréscimo para a impressora And CrSigCdOpe.EPrecos # 1
		Select crTpmMvItn
		Goto Bottom

		Replace crTpmMvItn.Units With ThisForm.CntItem.Cnt_Subtotal.Get_Valor.Value in crTpmMvItn
		
		Select crTpmMvItn
		_cIt = crTpmMvItn.citens

		Thisform.Total_Item()
		
		Thisform.ParentForm.TotIte = fTotalizarItn(ThisForm.parentform.Podatamgr)
		Thisform.CntItem.Get_Total.Value = Thisform.ParentForm.TotIte
		
		Select crTpmMvItn
		Locate for citens = _cit

		Select crTpmMvItn
		Replace ValDescs With Abs(This.Value)*-1
		*** PAF - Tiago - 15/03/2011
		Replace pDescs With Abs(lnpDesc)*-1 in crTpmMvItn			

		If InList( Thisform.op_escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) and CrSigCdOpe.EPrecos # 1 && PAF - Tiago - 15/03/2011 - Checagem de altera preço que estava acima foi colocada aqui
			If !Thisform.ItemFiscal()
				If Thisform.Cancelcupom()
					Thisform.mExcluiItem()
					ThisForm.Total_item()
					llOk = .f.
				Else
					Select crTpmMvItn
					Replace ImpCupFis with .t.
					ThisForm.AtuaCupom()
					ThisForm.ParentForm.CancelaItem = .T.
					ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Apaga.Enabled = .T.
				EndIf
			Else
				Select crTpmMvItn
				Replace ImpCupFis with .t.
				ThisForm.AtuaCupom()
				ThisForm.ParentForm.CancelaItem = .T.
				ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Apaga.Enabled = .T.
			Endif
			
			Thisform.mLimpaCntItem( .T. )
			Thisform.CntItem.cnt_produto.Get_Produto.SetFocus
		Endif
	Endif

	If (This.Value >= .Get_Unival.Value And This.Value # 0) or This.Value < 0 
		llOk = .F.
		Messagebox('Valor ou Percentual Inválido !!',0+48,'')
		Thisform.mExcluiItem()
		Thisform.mLimpaCntItem( .T. )
		Thisform.CntItem.cnt_produto.Get_Produto.SetFocus
	Endif

	If ( CrSigCdOpe.EPrecos # 1 ) And ( Not llOk Or .Get_Unival.Value # 0 )
		Thisform.mLimpaCntItem()
		Thisform.CntItem.cnt_produto.Get_Produto.SetFocus
	EndIf
EndWith

	Return .f.
Endif
If This.Value < 0
	This.Value = 0
	Return 0

If This.Value > 0 And This.Value # ThisForm.AntValue
	nAntValor = Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value + This.Value
	IF Not ThisForm.ParentForm.Chklimite(crTpmMvItn.Units,nAntValor,0,0,.t.)	&& mena - 22/03/2016 - foi trocada a ordem dos parametros

* OBJETO: SIGMVITN.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.get_pValAcre

PROCEDURE LostFocus
Thisform.Barra_Botoes.Altpreço.Visible = .f.

------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local lnValor
lnKey = Lastkey()
If Not ( lnKey = 13 Or lnKey = -2 Or lnKey = -4 )
	Return .f.
Endif
If This.Value < 0 && PAF - Tiago - 15/03/2011 - Não pode ter valor negativo
	This.Value = 0
	Return .F.
EndIf

If !(InList(crSigFiFis.Imps,'DATAREGIS','NDATAREGIS') And CrSigCdOpe.ChkEDesc = 1 And CrSigCdOpe.chkDVal = 1)
	If Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value > 0
		If This.Value > 0
			lnValor = Round((Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value * This.Value) /100,2 )
			nAntValor = Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value + lnValor
			IF Not ThisForm.ParentForm.Chklimite(crTpmMvItn.Units,nAntValor,0,0,.t.)	&& mena 22/03/2016 foi trocada a ordem dos parametros
				Select crTpmMvItn
				This.value = 0
				RETURN 0
			EndIf
			With Thisform.CntItem.cnt_desconto.Get_valAcre
				.Value = lnValor
				.Refresh
			EndWith
			With Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_SubTotal.Get_valor
				.Value = Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value + Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_desconto.Get_valAcre.Value
				.Refresh
				Replace crTpmMvItn.Units With .Value in crTpmMvItn
			EndWith
		Endif
	Endif
Else
	If Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value > 0
		If This.Value > 0
			lnValor   = (Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value * This.Value) /100
			nAntValor = (Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value + lnValor )
			IF Not ThisForm.ParentForm.Chklimite(crTpmMvItn.Units,nAntValor,0,0,.t.)	&& mena 03/05/2017 foi trocada a ordem dos parametros
				Select crTpmMvItn
				This.value = 0
				RETURN 0
			EndIf
			With Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_valAcre
				.Value = lnValor
				.Refresh
			EndWith
			With Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_SubTotal.Get_valor
				.Value = Thisform.CntItem.Cnt_Desconto.Get_Unival.Value + Thisform.CntItem.cnt_desconto.Get_valAcre.Value
				.Refresh
				Replace crTpmMvItn.Units With .Value in crTpmMvItn
			EndWith
		Endif
	Endif
Endif
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Thisform.Barra_Botoes.Altpreço.Visible = (ThisForm.lAlteraPreco)
llOk = ( InList( Thisform.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) And Not Thisform.lAlteraValor And ;
		Not Empty( Thisform.CntItem.cnt_produto.Get_qtds.Value) And ;
		CrSigCdOpe.ChkDVal = 1 And CrSigCdOpe.OpCrs # 4 And ;
		Empty(This.parent.Get_ValDesc.Value) )
If llOk
	Keyboard Chr(24)
Endif
Return llOk

ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGMVITN.CntFinanc.Cnt_Barras.Get_Barra_1
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 62
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
If Empty(This.Value) And Lastkey() = 13
	ThisForm.CntFinanc.Proximo.SetFocus
EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
If Not Empty(This.Value)
	lcCodigo = Left(This.Value,16)
	lcCodigo = lcCodigo + CalcDv(lcCodigo)
	lcCodigo = lcCodigo + CalcDv(lcCodigo)

	If Not lcCodigo = Alltrim(This.Value)
		MessageBox('Barra Inválido !!!', 16, [])
		Return(.F.)
	EndIf
EndIf

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGMVITN.CntFinanc.Cnt_Barras.Get_Barra_2
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 63
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
ThisForm.CntFinanc.Cnt_Barras.Get_Barra_1.SetFocus

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
If Not Empty(This.Value)

	lcBarraA = Alltrim(This.parent.Get_Barra_1.Value)
	lnParcA  = Val(Substr(lcBarraA,9,2))
	lcCodigo = Left(This.Value,16)
	lcCodigo = lcCodigo + CalcDv(lcBarraA + lcCodigo)
	lcCodigo = lcCodigo + CalcDv(lcBarraA + lcCodigo)

	lnEmp  = Val(Left(lcBarraA,3))
	lnDop  = Val(Substr(lcBarraA,4,5))
	lnNum  = Val(Substr(lcBarraA,11,6))
	lnParc = Val(Left(This.Value, 2))
	ldVenc = Ctod(Sys(10, Val(Substr(This.Value, 3, 7))))
	lnValo = Val(Substr(This.Value, 10, 7)) / 100
	
	If Not lcCodigo = Alltrim(This.Value) Or IsEmpty(ldVenc) Or lnParcA <> lnParc
		MessageBox('Barra Inválido!!!', 16, [])
	Else
		Select TmpBarFin
		Locate For nBarra1 = Val(lcBarraA) And nBarra2 = Val(This.Value)
		If Not Eof()
			MessageBox('Barra ja informado!!!',0+16,'')
		Else
			Insert Into TmpBarFin (nBarra1,nBarra2,nEmps,nParcs,ValPs,VencPs,nDopes,nNumes) Values ;
				(Val(lcBarraA),Val(This.Value),lnEmp,lnParc,lnValo,ldVenc,lnDop,lnNum)
		Endif
	Endif
	This.Parent.Get_Barra_1.Value = ''
	This.Parent.Get_Barra_2.Value = ''

	This.Parent.Get_Barra_1.Refresh
	This.Parent.Get_Barra_2.Refresh
	
	ThisForm.CntFinanc.Cnt_GrdBarra.Grade.Refresh

EndIf
ThisForm.ParentForm.Atuatot()
ThisForm.CntFinanc.Get_total.Refresh

Return .t.
ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGMVITN.Cnt_Credito.GradeDevol.Column1.get_Devol
* Classe: textbox (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 73
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
Set Confirm Off
If This.Value = 0 And Thisform.peantvalue = 0 And Lastkey() = 13 And TmpDevol.Saida
	lcFoco = 'ThisForm.Cnt_Credito.Proximo.SetFocus'
	&lcFoco.
Else
	This.SetFocus
Endif

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local nDope, Nume, CodConf, _Posicao, NOpCr, lcNotas, lcProcura, lnCfgOpCrs, lnBxVlOpCrs, lnCfgChkCt
Local _ValDev, _ValNDev, lcFpagOpCrs, lnCotOpe, lnCotPar, lcCMoeOpCrs, llNovaLinha, lnRecNo
Local lcOutros, llRecalcula, llRetorno, lcMsg, lcCaption, _nddVal

ThisForm.Validado = .t.
This.Refresh
If This.Value # Thisform.peantvalue
	If This.Value # 0
		If Thisform.peantvalue # 0
			This.Value = Thisform.peantvalue
			Return .t.
		Endif
		Skip
		Skip -1
		Select sum(1) as Qt, Codigos From TmpDevol ;
			Where Codigos = This.Value Into Cursor CheckDup GROUP by 2
		Select CheckDup
		Go Top
		If CheckDup.Qt > 1 
			MessageBox('Movimentação já digitada !!!',0+48,'')
			This.Value = 0
			Select TmpDevol
			Return 0
		Endif
	EndIf
Else
	If This.Value = 0
		Replace Saida With .t. in TmpDevol
	Endif
	Return .t.
Endif

llRecalcula = .F.
llNovaLinha = .F.
llRetorno   = .T.
Select TprMvCab
Go Top
_Posicao = TprMvCab.emps + TprMvCab.Dopes + str( TprMvCab.numes, 6 )
=ThisForm.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdOpe','CrSigCdOpe','dopes',TprMvCab.Dopes)
NOpCr       = crSigCdOpe.NOpCrs
lnCfgOpCrs  = crSigCdOpe.OpCrs
lnBxVlOpCrs = crSigCdOpe.BxVlOpCrs
lcFPagOpCrs = crSigCdOpe.FPagOpCrs
lnCfgChkCt  = CrSigCdOpe.Consgs
lcCMoeOpCrs = .F.
_ValDev     = 0
_ValNDev    = 0
_PuxaOrigem = crSigCdOpe.ConOSubs
_PuxaDestino= crSigCdOpe.ConDSubs
_nddVal = 0

If Not Empty( ThisForm.PeAntValue )

	Set Decimals to 0
	nDope = Val( Left( Str( Thisform.peantvalue, 10), 4 ) )

	lcQuery = [Select * From SigCdOpe Where nDopes = ]+Str(nDope)
	=Thisform.PodataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'CrSigCdOpe')

	Dope = crSigCdOpe.Dopes
	Nume = Val( Right( Str( Thisform.peantvalue, 10 ), 6 ) )
	lcNotas = Transf(Nume, '@L 999999') 
	set decimals to 2
	lcOutros = ''
	If lnCfgOpCrs = 1
		lcSql = [Select * From SigMvCab Where EmpdopNums = ']+TprMvCab.Emps + Dope + Str(Nume,6)+[']
	Else
		If lnCfgOpCrs = 3
			lcSql = [Select * From SigMvCab Where Dopes = ']+Dope+[' And Notas = ']+lcNotas+[']
		Else
			Loop
		EndIf
	EndIf

	=ThisForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcSql,'CrTprMvCab')

	If Used('CrTprMvCab') And Reccount('CrTprMvCab') > 0
		lcSubn = .f.
		ldDtAlt= Datetime()
		llDtBx = Null
		lcUpDate = [UpDate SigMvCab Set ChkSubn = ?lcSubn, DtAlts = ?ldDtAlt, DtBaixas = Null, DataTrans = Null Where CidChaves = ']+CrTprMvCab.CidChaves+[']
		lnErro = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcUpDate,'')
		If LnErro < 0
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão (Update SigMvCab 3)')
			Return 0
		EndIf
		lcOutros = CrTprMvCab.Emps + ' : ' + Iif(lnCfgOpCrs = 1, Transf(CrTprMvCab.Numes,'@L 999999'), crTprMvCab.Notas )
		_ValDev  = Iif( lnBxVlOpCrs = 2, 0, CrTprMvCab.Valos - CrTprMvCab.ValoBxs )
		_ValNDev = CrTprMvCab.Valos - CrTprMvCab.ValoBxs
		llRecalcula = .T.
		llNovaLinha = .T.
	Else
		MessageBox('Dados Inconsistentes ... ', 48, 'Operação Não Encontrada')
		=ThisForm.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdOpe','CrSigCdOpe','dopes',TprMvCab.Dopes)
		This.Value = 0
		Return 0
	EndIf

	=Thisform.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigOpFp','CrSigOpFp','Fpags',lcFpagOpCrs)
		
	If Not IsEmpty(lcFPagOpCrs)
		Select xPar
		Delete All For EmpDopNums = CrTprMvCab.EmpDopNums And Fpags = lcFPagOpCrs

		Select xParTef
		Delete All For EmpDopNums = CrTprMvCab.EmpDopNums And Fpags = lcFPagOpCrs
	EndIf

	Select [ ] as Agrupar, sum(1) as Qt From TmpOperacao ;
		Where Codigos > 0 Into Cursor CheckDup Group by 1
	If CheckDup.Qt = 0
		Select [ ] as Agrupar,sum(1) as Qt From TmpDevol ;
			Where Codigos > 0 Into Cursor CheckDup2 Group by 1
		If CheckDup2.Qt = 0
			ThisForm.Editadestino = .t.
			ThisForm.EditaOrigem  = .t.
		Endif
	EndIf

EndIf

=ThisForm.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdOpe','CrSigCdOpe','dopes',TprMvCab.Dopes)

Select TmpDevol
If This.Value = 0
	Replace Saida With .t. In TmpDevol
	Return .T.
Endif

Set Decimals to 0
NDope = Val( Left( Str( This.Value, 10 ), 4 ) )		&& Número da Operação
If Not IsEmpty(NDope)
	=ThisForm.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdOpe','CrSigCdOpe','ndopes',nDope)
	Select crSigCdOpe
	If Reccount('CrSigCdOpe') > 0
		lcSql = [Select Dopes, nddVals From SigOpCdc Where Dopes = ']+CrSigCdOpe.Dopes+[']
		ThisForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcSql,'LocalDcOpe')

		Dope    = crSigCdOpe.Dopes
		Nume    = Val( Right( Str( This.Value, 10 ), 6 ) )
		lcNotas = Transf(Nume, '@L 999999') 
		lcCMoeOpCrs = crSigCdOpe.CMoes
		_nDdVal = LocalDcOpe.nDDvals

		Set Decimals to 2
		If Nume # 0
			Declare LaCampo(2,2)
			LaCampo(1,1)='Dopes'
			LaCampo(2,1)='Notas'
			LaCampo(1,2)=Dope
			LaCampo(2,2)=lcNotas

			lcBusca = TprMvCab.Emps + Dope + Str(Nume,6)

			Do Case
				Case NOpCr # Dope
					MessageBox('Operação de Crédito Inválida!!',0+48,'')
					llRetorno = .F.
						
				Case lnCfgOpCrs = 1 AND NOT ThisForm.podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigMvCab','CrEest','EmpDopNums',lcbusca)
					MessageBox('Movimentação Inválida !!!',0+48,'')
					llRetorno = .F.

				Case lnCfgOpCrs = 1 And lnCfgChkCt # 2 AND ThisForm.podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigMvCab','CrEest','EmpDopNums',lcbusca);
						 AND (TprMvCab.GrupoDs+TprMvCab.Contads) # (crEest.Grupoos+crEest.Contaos)
					MessageBox('Conta Inválida!!',0+48,'')
					llRetorno = .F.
						
				Case lnCfgOpCrs = 3 AND NOT ThisForm.podatamgr.Cursorquery('SigMvCab','CrEest',@LaCampo)
					MessageBox('Movimentação: ' + AllTrim(Dope) + ' ' + lcNotas + ;
							   ' Não Encontrada.', 48, 'Operação de Crédito Não Encontrada')
					llRetorno = .F.
						
				Case CrEest.ChkSubN
					lcMsg = Iif(lnCfgOpCrs = 3,'Crédito: ' + AllTrim(crEest.Dopes) + ' ' + crEest.Notas, ;
							'Movimentação: ' + AllTrim(crEest.Emps) + ' ' + AllTrim(crEest.Dopes) + ' ' + AllTrim(Str(crEest.Numes,6)))
					lcMsg = lcMsg + ' Já Utilizado.'
					lcCaption = Iif(lnCfgOpCrs = 3,'Crédito já utilizado','Movimentação já utilizada')
					MessageBox(lcMsg, 48, lcCaption)
					llRetorno = .F.

			Other

				If _nDdval > 0 And Ttod(CrEest.Datas) + _nDdVal < Ttod(TprMvCab.Datas)
					Do Form SigOpSen With 'DTVALIDA', Proper( CrEest.Dopes ) To _Retorno
					_Retorna = ( Left( _Retorno, 1 ) = "*" )
					If Not _Retorna
						Select TmpDevol
						This.Value = 0
						Return 0
					Endif
				EndIf
				lcMsg = ThisForm.ValidaCertficado(CrEest.Status, ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr)
				If Not Empty(lcMsg)
					=MessageBox(lcMsg, 0+16, [Validação de Utilização de SubNivel])
					Select TmpDevol
					This.Value = 0
					Return 0
				EndIf

				=ThisForm.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdOpe','CrSigCdOpe','dopes',TprMvCab.Dopes)

				llNovaLinha = .T.
				llRecalcula = .T.
				lcSubn = .t.
				ldDtAlt= Datetime()
				llDtBx = TprMvCab.Datas

				lcUpDate = [UpDate SigMvCab Set ChkBxParcs = ?lcSubn Where CidChaves = ']+CrEest.CidChaves+[']
				lnErro = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcUpDate,'')
				If LnErro < 0
					=MessageBox('Registro em Uso por Outro Usuario', 16, 'Falha na Conexão ')
					Select TmpDevol
					This.Value = 0
					Return 0
				EndIf
					
				lcSql = [Select ChkSubn From SigMvCab Where CidChaves = ']+CrEest.CidChaves+[']
				lnErro = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'LocalEest')
				If LnErro < 0
					MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão (LocalEest)')
					Select TmpDevol
					This.Value = 0
					Return 0
				EndIf
					
				If LocalEest.ChkSubN
					lcMsg = Iif(lnCfgOpCrs = 3,'Crédito: ' + AllTrim(crEest.Dopes) + ' ' + crEest.Notas, ;
							'Movimentação: ' + AllTrim(crEest.Emps) + ' ' + AllTrim(crEest.Dopes) + ' ' + AllTrim(Str(crEest.Numes,6)))
					lcMsg = lcMsg + ' Já Utilizado.'
					lcCaption = Iif(lnCfgOpCrs = 3,'Crédito já utilizado','Movimentação já utilizada')
					MessageBox(lcMsg, 48, lcCaption)
					Select TmpDevol
					This.Value = 0
					Return 0
				Endif

				lcUpDate = [UpDate SigMvCab Set ChkSubn = ?lcSubn, DtAlts = ?ldDtAlt, DtBaixas = ?llDtBx Where CidChaves = ']+CrEest.CidChaves+[']
				lnErro = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcUpDate,'')
				If LnErro < 0
					=MessageBox('Registro em Uso por Outro Usuario', 16, 'Falha na Conexão ')
					Select TmpDevol
					This.Value = 0
					Return 0
				EndIf

				_ValDev  = Iif( lnBxVlOpCrs = 2, 0, creest.Valos - CrEest.ValoBxs)
				_ValNDev = ( CrEest.Valos - CrEest.ValoBxs )
				=Thisform.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigOpFp','CrSigOpFp','Fpags',lcFpagOpCrs)
				If Not IsEmpty(lcFPagOpCrs) And lnBxVlOpCrs = 2 And Type('lcCMoeOpCrs') = 'C' And CrSigOpFp.nCodFinzs > 0
					lcOutros = crEest.Emps + ' : ' + Iif(lnCfgOpCrs = 1, Transf(crEest.Numes,'@L 999999'), crEest.Notas )

					Insert Into xPar ( FPags, Emps, Dopes, Numes, Datas, Parcs, Vencs, ;
									   InsChs, Empos, Outros, EmpdopNums ) Values ;
									 ( lcFPagOpCrs, TprMvCab.Emps, TprMvCab.Dopes, TprMvCab.Numes, ;
									 	TprMvCab.Datas, 1, TprMvCab.Datas, .T., TprMvCab.Emps, ;
									 	lcOutros, CrEest.EmpdopNums )

					lnCotOpe = ThisForm.parentform.Cotacao( lcCMoeOpCrs, TprMvCab.Datas )
					lnCotPar = ThisForm.parentform.Cotacao( crSigOpFp.MoeFPgs, TprMvCab.Datas )

					Select xPar
					Replace xPar.Valos With Round( _ValNDev * lnCotOpe / lnCotPar, 2), ;
							xPar.VPags With Iif(crSigOpFp.ValPends = 2, ;
											Round( _ValNDev * lnCotOpe / lnCotPar, 2), 0 ), ;
							xPar.MoeFPgs With crSigOpFp.MoeFPgs, ;
							xPar.CotFPgs With lnCotPar in xPar
								
					Select xParTef
					Set Order To Fpags
					If Not Seek(CrEest.EmpDopNums + lcFPagOpCrs)
						Append Blank
						Replace EmpDopNums With CrEest.EmpDopNums,;
								Fpags      With lcFPagOpCrs,;
								Utilizado  with .t.,;
								Vencs      With Date(),;
								nParcs     With 1,;
								Creditos   With .t. in xParTef
					EndIf
					Replace Valos With xPar.Valos In xParTef

					=ThisForm.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdOpe','CrSigCdOpe','dopes',TprMvCab.Dopes)

					If InList(_PuxaDestino,1,2)
						If IsEmpty( TprMvCab.Grupods ) Or ( IsEmpty( TprMvCab.Contads ) And !crSigCdOpe.FixGDests )
							ThisForm.Parentform.Pagina.Dados.Destino.Get_Grupo.Value = crEest.Grupoos
						Endif
						If IsEmpty( TprMvCab.Contads ) Or (TprMvCab.Contads = CrSigCdOpe.ConDests)
							ThisForm.Parentform.Pagina.Dados.Destino.Get_Conta.Value = crEest.Contaos
						EndIf
						Thisform.EditaDestino = .f.
					EndIf
				Endif
			Endcase
		Else
			This.Value = 0
		Endif
	Else
		If Not IsEmpty(nDope)
			MessageBox('Número De Operação: ' + Transf(nDope,'99') + ;
					   ' não está Cadastrado no Sistema.' + Chr(13) + ;
					   'Contate o Suporte Técnico para corrigir a configuração.', 48, ;
					   'Erro de Configuração do Sistema')
		EndIf
		This.Value = 0
	EndIf

Else
	If Not IsEmpty(This.Value) And This.Value # Thisform.peantvalue 
		MessageBox('Código de Operação de Crédito ' + Transf(This.Value,'@L 99999999') + ;
				   ' é Inválido ... ',48, 'Erro')
		This.Value = 0
		=ThisForm.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdOpe','CrSigCdOpe','dopes',TprMvCab.Dopes)
		Return 0
	EndIf
EndIf

If llRecalcula
	Thisform.parentform.TotIte = fTotalizarItn(ThisForm.Podatamgr)
	lnIpi = 0
	Select CrSigMvNfi
	Locate For EmpDopNums = TprMvCab.Emps + TprMvCab.Dopes + Str( TprMvCab.Numes, 6 )
	If !Eof()
		If !crSigMvNfi.Cancelas
			lnIpi = crSigMvNfi.ValIpis
		EndIf
	Endif

	Thisform.parentform.nValDevols = _ValDev
	Select TprMvCab
	Replace ValDevs  With ValDevs + _ValDev,;
			ValNDevs With ValNDevs + _ValNDev

	Replace ValInis  With Thisform.parentform.TotIte - TprMvCab.ValDevs, ;
			Valos    With Round( Thisform.parentform.totite - TprMvCab.ValDevs + ;
						         TprMvCab.ValVars + TprMvCab.ValServs + lnIPI, 2 )

	ThisForm.parentform.Totpar

EndIf

=ThisForm.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdOpe','CrSigCdOpe','dopes',TprMvCab.Dopes)

Select TmpDevol
If Not llRetorno
	This.Value = 0
	Return 0
EndIf

If llNovaLinha
	Select TmpDevol
	Locate For Codigos = 0
	If Eof()
		Insert Into TmpDevol (Emps, Dopes, Numes, Codigos, TpCodigos) ;
				Values (TprMvCab.Emps, TprMvCab.Dopes, TprMvCab.Numes, 0, lnCfgOpCrs)
	EndIf
	KeyBoard "{DNARROW}"
EndIf

Select [ ] as Agrupar, sum(1) as Qt From TmpDevol ;
	Where Codigos > 0 Into Cursor CheckDup Group by 1
If CheckDup.Qt = 0
	Select [ ] as Agrupar, sum(1) as Qt From TmpOperacao ;
		Where Codigos > 0 Into Cursor CheckDup2 group by 1
	If CheckDup2.Qt = 0
		ThisForm.Editadestino = .t.
		ThisForm.EditaOrigem  = .t.
	Endif
EndIf

ThisForm.Cnt_Credito.Refresh 
Select TmpDevol
Return .t.

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Set Confirm on
ThisForm.Validado = .f.
ThisForm.peantvalue = This.Value


ENDPROC

	ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Itens.Click()
Else
	ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Pagto.SetFocus
	ThisForm.Barra_Botoes.Pagto.Click()
Endif

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE GotFocus
Select [ ] as Agrupar, sum(1) as Qt From TprMvCabV Where Not Empty(Contas) Into Cursor TotEestV group by 1
If TotEestv.Qt > 0
	ThisForm.Cnt_identifica.Vendedor.Get_conta.Value = ' '
	ThisForm.Cnt_identifica.Vendedor.get_cpf.Value = ' '
	ThisForm.Cnt_identifica.Vendedor.get_dconta.Value = ' '
EndIf
ThisForm.Cnt_pendencia.Proximo.picture = '\vbmp\b_arrow4.bmp'
ENDPROC
